+308|function hashPassword(password) {
+309|  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(password).digest('hex');
+310|}
+311|
+312|function generateToken() {
+313|  return crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
+314|}
+315|
+316|function hashOperationInput(method, path, input) {
+317|  const key = `${method}:${path}:${JSON.stringify(input, 
Object.keys(input).sort())}`;
+318|  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(key).digest('hex');
+319|}
+320|
+321|function getCurrentUser(token) {
+322|  if (!token) return null;
+323|  const session = db.prepare('SELECT user_id FROM sessions WHERE token = 
?').get(token);
+324|  if (!session) return null;
+325|  return db.prepare('SELECT id, name, email, role FROM users WHERE id = 
?').get(session.user_id);
+326|}
+327|
+328|function logAudit(courseId, actorId, eventType, resourceType, resourceId, 
details) {
+329|  db.prepare(`
+330|    INSERT INTO audit (course_id, actor_id, event_type, resource_type, 
resource_id, details, timestamp)
+331|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+332|  `).run(courseId, actorId, eventType, resourceType, resourceId, 
JSON.stringify(details), REFERENCE_MOMENT.toISOString());
+333|}
+334|
+335|function memoizeReceipt(user, operationId, method, path, input, status, 
response) {
+336|  const inputHash = hashOperationInput(method, path, input);
+337|  db.prepare(`
+338|    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO operation_receipts (user_id, operation_id, 
method, path, input_hash, status, response_json, created_at)
+339|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+340|  `).run(user.id, operationId, method, path, inputHash, status, 
JSON.stringify(response), REFERENCE_MOMENT.toISOString());
+341|}
+342|
+343|function getReceipt(user, operationId, method, path, input) {
+344|  const inputHash = hashOperationInput(method, path, input);
+345|  return db.prepare(`
+346|    SELECT status, response_json FROM operation_receipts 
+347|    WHERE user_id = ? AND operation_id = ? AND method = ? AND path = ? AND 
input_hash = ?
+348|  `).get(user.id, operationId, method, path, inputHash);
+349|}

+351|function getCourseRevision(courseId) {
+352|  const result = db.prepare('SELECT revision FROM courses WHERE id = 
?').get(courseId);
+353|  return result ? result.revision : null;
+354|}
+355|
+356|function incrementCourseRevision(courseId) {
+357|  db.prepare('UPDATE courses SET revision = revision + 1 WHERE id = 
?').run(courseId);
+358|  return getCourseRevision(courseId);

+365|// Authentication routes
+366|app.post('/api/auth/sign-in', (req, res) => {
+367|  const { email, password } = req.body;
+368|  
+369|  if (!email || !password) {
+370|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email and password required' });
+371|  }
+372|  
+373|  const user = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, email, role FROM users WHERE 
email = ?').get(email);
+374|  if (!user || hashPassword(password) !== db.prepare('SELECT password_hash 
FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(user.id).password_hash) {
+375|    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid credentials' });
+376|  }
+377|  
+378|  const token = generateToken();
+379|  db.prepare('INSERT INTO sessions (user_id, token, created_at) VALUES (?, 
?, ?)').run(user.id, token, REFERENCE_MOMENT.toISOString());
+380|  
+381|  res.json({ token, user: { id: user.id, name: user.name, email: 
user.email, role: user.role } });
+382|});
+383|
+384|app.post('/api/auth/sign-out', (req, res) => {
+385|  const token = req.headers.authorization?.split(' ')[1];
+386|  const user = getCurrentUser(token);
+387|  
+388|  if (!user) {
+389|    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Not authenticated' });
+390|  }
+391|  
+392|  db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ?').run(user.id);
+393|  res.json({ success: true });
+394|});

+401|// Protected middleware
+402|function requireAuth(req, res, next) {
+403|  const token = req.headers.authorization?.split(' ')[1];
+404|  const user = getCurrentUser(token);
+405|  
+406|  if (!user) {
+407|    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Not authenticated' });
+408|  }
+409|  
+410|  req.user = user;
+411|  next();
+412|}

+425|    )

+433|  
+434|  const hasAccess = db.prepare(`
+435|    SELECT 1 FROM enrollments WHERE course_id = ? AND user_id = ?
+436|    UNION

+438|  `).get(courseId, req.user.id, courseId, req.user.id);
+439|

+448|// Assessments
+449|app.get('/api/assessments/:courseId', (req, res) => {
+450|  const { courseId } = req.params;
+451|  
+452|  const hasAccess = db.prepare(`
+453|    SELECT 1 FROM enrollments WHERE course_id = ? AND user_id = ?
+454|    UNION
+455|    SELECT 1 FROM courses WHERE id = ? AND instructor_id = ?
+456|  `).get(courseId, req.user.id, courseId, req.user.id);
+457|  
+458|  if (!hasAccess) {
+459|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Not enrolled' });
+460|  }
+461|  
+462|  let query = 'SELECT id, title, status, opens_at, due_at, 
duration_minutes, max_attempts FROM assessments WHERE course_id = ?';
+463|  const params = [courseId];
+464|  
+465|  if (req.user.role === 'student') {
+466|    query += ' AND status = ?';
+467|    params.push('published');
+468|  }
+469|  
+470|  const assessments = db.prepare(query).all(...params);
+471|  res.json(assessments);
+472|});

+474|app.get('/api/assessments/:courseId/:assessmentId', (req, res) => {
+475|  const { courseId, assessmentId } = req.params;
+476|  
+477|  const hasAccess = db.prepare(`
+478|    SELECT 1 FROM enrollments WHERE course_id = ? AND user_id = ?
+479|    UNION
+480|    SELECT 1 FROM courses WHERE id = ? AND instructor_id = ?
+481|  `).get(courseId, req.user.id, courseId, req.user.id);
+482|  
+483|  if (!hasAccess) {
+484|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Not enrolled' });
+485|  }
+486|  
+487|  const assessment = db.prepare(`
+488|    SELECT id, title, status, opens_at, due_at, duration_minutes, 
max_attempts FROM assessments 
+489|    WHERE id = ? AND course_id = ?
+490|  `).get(assessmentId, courseId);
+491|  
+492|  if (!assessment) {
+493|    return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Assessment not found' });
+494|  }
+495|  
+496|  if (req.user.role === 'student' && assessment.status !== 'published') {
+497|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Not available' });
+498|  }
+499|  
+500|  const items = db.prepare('SELECT id, kind, prompt, points FROM items 
WHERE assessment_id = ?').all(assessmentId);
+501|  
+502|  const itemsWithRubrics = items.map(item => {
+503|    const rubric = db.prepare('SELECT criteria_json FROM rubrics WHERE 
item_id = ?').get(item.id);
+504|    return {
+505|      ...item,
+506|      rubric: rubric ? JSON.parse(rubric.criteria_json) : null
+507|    };
+508|  });
+509|  
+510|  if (req.user.role === 'student') {
+511|    itemsWithRubrics.forEach(item => {
+512|      if (item.kind === 'multiple_choice') {
+513|        const options = db.prepare('SELECT options FROM items WHERE id = 
?').get(item.id);
+514|        item.options = options ? JSON.parse(options.options) : [];
+515|        delete item.rubric;
+516|      }
+517|    });
+518|  } else {
+519|    itemsWithRubrics.forEach(item => {
+520|      const dbItem = db.prepare('SELECT options, answer FROM items WHERE id
= ?').get(item.id);
+521|      if (item.kind === 'multiple_choice' && dbItem) {
+522|        item.options = dbItem.options ? JSON.parse(dbItem.options) : [];
+523|        item.answer = dbItem.answer;
+524|      }
+525|    });
+526|  }
+527|  
+528|  res.json({ ...assessment, items: itemsWithRubrics });
+529|});
+530|
+531|// Assessment authoring (instructor only)
+532|app.post('/api/assessments/:courseId/create-draft', (req, res) => {
+533|  const { courseId } = req.params;
+534|  const { operationId, title, opens_at, due_at, duration_minutes, 
max_attempts } = req.body;
+535|  
+536|  if (req.user.role !== 'instructor') {
+537|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Instructor only' });
+538|  }
+539|  
+540|  const course = db.prepare('SELECT instructor_id, revision FROM courses 
WHERE id = ?').get(courseId);
+541|  if (!course || course.instructor_id !== req.user.id) {
+542|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Not authorized' });
+543|  }
+544|  
+545|  // Check receipt
+546|  const receipt = getReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/create-draft`, req.body);
+547|  if (receipt) {
+548|    return 
res.status(receipt.status).json(JSON.parse(receipt.response_json));
+549|  }
+550|  
+551|  // Validate input
+552|  const errors = [];
+553|  if (!title || typeof title !== 'string' || title.trim() === '') 
errors.push('Title required');
+554|  if (!opens_at || isNaN(new Date(opens_at).getTime())) errors.push('Valid 
opening time required');
+555|  if (!due_at || isNaN(new Date(due_at).getTime())) errors.push('Valid due 
time required');
+556|  if (new Date(opens_at) >= new Date(due_at)) errors.push('Opening must be 
before due time');
+557|  if (!Number.isInteger(duration_minutes) || duration_minutes <= 0) 
errors.push('Positive whole minute duration required');
+558|  if (!Number.isInteger(max_attempts) || max_attempts <= 0) 
errors.push('Positive attempt limit required');
+559|  
+560|  if (errors.length > 0) {
+561|    const response = { errors };
+562|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/create-draft`, req.body, 400, response);
+563|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+564|  }
+565|  
+566|  const assessmentId = 'A-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 
9).toUpperCase();
+567|  
+568|  db.prepare(`
+569|    INSERT INTO assessments (id, course_id, title, status, opens_at, 
due_at, duration_minutes, max_attempts)
+570|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+571|  `).run(assessmentId, courseId, title, 'draft', opens_at, due_at, 
duration_minutes, max_attempts);
+572|  
+573|  const response = { id: assessmentId, status: 'draft', title, opens_at, 
due_at, duration_minutes, max_attempts };
+574|  memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/create-draft`, req.body, 201, response);
+575|  res.status(201).json(response);
+576|});
+577|
+578|app.post('/api/assessments/:courseId/:assessmentId/add-item', (req, res) =>
{
+579|  const { courseId, assessmentId } = req.params;
+580|  const { operationId, kind, prompt, options, answer, points } = req.body;
+581|  
+582|  if (req.user.role !== 'instructor') {
+583|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Instructor only' });
+584|  }
+585|  
+586|  const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT status, course_id FROM assessments 
WHERE id = ?').get(assessmentId);
+587|  if (!assessment || assessment.course_id !== courseId) {
+588|    return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Assessment not found' });
+589|  }
+590|  
+591|  const course = db.prepare('SELECT instructor_id FROM courses WHERE id = 
?').get(courseId);
+592|  if (!course || course.instructor_id !== req.user.id) {
+593|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Not authorized' });
+594|  }
+595|  
+596|  if (assessment.status !== 'draft') {
+597|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Can only add items to draft' });
+598|  }
+599|  
+600|  // Check receipt
+601|  const receipt = getReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}/add-item`, req.body);
+602|  if (receipt) {
+603|    return 
res.status(receipt.status).json(JSON.parse(receipt.response_json));
+604|  }
+605|  
+606|  // Validate
+607|  const errors = [];
+608|  if (!prompt || typeof prompt !== 'string' || prompt.trim() === '') 
errors.push('Prompt required');
+609|  if (!Number.isInteger(points) || points <= 0) errors.push('Positive 
points required');
+610|  if (kind === 'multiple_choice') {
+611|    if (!Array.isArray(options) || options.length < 2) errors.push('At 
least two options required');
+612|    else {
+613|      const trimmed = options.map(o => (typeof o === 'string' ? o.trim() : 
'')).filter(o => o);
+614|      const unique = new Set(trimmed);
+615|      if (trimmed.length !== unique.size) errors.push('Options must be 
unique');
+616|      if (trimmed.length < 2) errors.push('At least two distinct options 
required');
+617|    }
+618|    if (!answer || !options.includes(answer)) errors.push('Answer must 
match an option');
+619|  }
+620|  
+621|  if (errors.length > 0) {
+622|    const response = { errors };
+623|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}/add-item`, req.body, 400, 
response);
+624|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+625|  }
+626|  
+627|  const itemId = 'I-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 
9).toUpperCase();
+628|  const optionsJson = kind === 'multiple_choice' ? JSON.stringify(options) 
: null;
+629|  
+630|  db.prepare(`
+631|    INSERT INTO items (id, assessment_id, kind, prompt, options, answer, 
points)
+632|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+633|  `).run(itemId, assessmentId, kind, prompt, optionsJson, answer || null, 
points);
+634|  
+635|  if (kind === 'written') {
+636|    const criteriaJson = JSON.stringify([
+637|      { id: `RC-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 5).toUpperCase()}`, 
label: 'Response quality', max_points: points }
+638|    ]);
+639|    db.prepare('INSERT INTO rubrics (item_id, criteria_json) VALUES (?, 
?)').run(itemId, criteriaJson);
+640|  }
+641|  
+642|  const response = { id: itemId, kind, prompt, options, answer, points };
+643|  memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}/add-item`, req.body, 201, 
response);
+644|  res.status(201).json(response);

+720|    return res.status(400).json(response);

+724|  const openTime = new Date(assessment.opens_at);
+725|  const dueTime = new Date(assessment.due_at);
+726|  
+727|  if (now < openTime) {

+735|  const deadlineExt = accommodation ?

+736|  
+737|  const effectiveDue = new Date(dueTime.getTime() + deadlineExt * 60000);
+738|

+740|    const response = { error: 'Assessment closed' };
+741|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST',

+742|    return res.status(400).json(response);

+748|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST',

+749|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+750|  }

+753|  if (attemptCount >= assessment.max_attempts) {
+754|    const response = { error: 'Attempt limit reached' };

+943|// Grading
+944|app.post('/api/attempts/:attemptId/grade', (req, res) => {
+945|  const { attemptId } = req.params;
+946|  const { operationId, expectedRevision, criterionId, score, feedback } = 
req.body;
+947|  
+948|  if (req.user.role === 'student') {
+949|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Cannot grade' });
+950|  }
+951|  
+952|  // Check receipt
+953|  const receipt = getReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade`, req.body);
+954|  if (receipt) {
+955|    return 
res.status(receipt.status).json(JSON.parse(receipt.response_json));
+956|  }
+957|  
+958|  const attempt = db.prepare('SELECT assessment_id, status, 
assigned_grader_id FROM attempts WHERE id = ?').get(attemptId);
+959|  
+960|  if (!attempt) {
+961|    const response = { error: 'Attempt not found' };
+962|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade`, req.body, 404, response);
+963|    return res.status(404).json(response);
+964|  }
+965|  
+966|  if (attempt.status !== 'submitted' && attempt.status !== 'graded') {
+967|    const response = { error: 'Attempt not gradeable' };
+968|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade`, req.body, 400, response);
+969|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+970|  }
+971|  
+972|  if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && attempt.assigned_grader_id 
!== req.user.id) {
+973|    const response = { error: 'Not assigned' };
+974|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade`, req.body, 403, response);
+975|    return res.status(403).json(response);
+976|  }
+977|  
+978|  if (!Number.isFinite(score) || score < 0) {
+979|    const response = { error: 'Invalid score' };
+980|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade`, req.body, 400, response);
+981|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+982|  }
+983|  
+984|  // Get max points for criterion
+985|  const items = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM items WHERE assessment_id = 
?').all(attempt.assessment_id);
+986|  let maxPoints = 0;
+987|  let foundCriterion = false;
+988|  
+989|  for (const item of items) {
+990|    const rubric = db.prepare('SELECT criteria_json FROM rubrics WHERE 
item_id = ?').get(item.id);
+991|    if (rubric) {
+992|      const criteria = JSON.parse(rubric.criteria_json);
+993|      for (const criterion of criteria) {
+994|        if (criterion.id === criterionId) {
+995|          maxPoints = criterion.max_points;
+996|          foundCriterion = true;
+997|          break;
+998|        }
+999|      }
+1000|    }
+1001|    if (foundCriterion) break;
+1002|  }
+1003|  
+1004|  if (!foundCriterion || score > maxPoints) {
+1005|    const response = { error: 'Invalid criterion or score out of range' };
+1006|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade`, req.body, 400, response);
+1007|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+1008|  }
+1009|  
+1010|  db.prepare(`
+1011|    INSERT OR REPLACE INTO rubric_grades (attempt_id, criterion_id, score,
feedback, graded_by, graded_at)
+1012|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1013|  `).run(attemptId, criterionId, score, feedback, req.user.id, 
REFERENCE_MOMENT.toISOString());
+1014|  
+1015|  // Check if all rubric criteria are graded
+1016|  let allGraded = true;
+1017|  for (const item of items) {
+1018|    if (item.kind === 'written') {
+1019|      const rubric = db.prepare('SELECT criteria_json FROM rubrics WHERE 
item_id = ?').get(item.id);
+1020|      if (rubric) {
+1021|        const criteria = JSON.parse(rubric.criteria_json);
+1022|        for (const criterion of criteria) {
+1023|          const grade = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM rubric_grades WHERE 
attempt_id = ? AND criterion_id = ?').get(attemptId, criterion.id);
+1024|          if (!grade) {
+1025|            allGraded = false;
+1026|            break;
+1027|          }
+1028|        }
+1029|      }
+1030|    }
+1031|    if (!allGraded) break;
+1032|  }
+1033|  
+1034|  if (allGraded) {
+1035|    const rubricGrades = db.prepare('SELECT score FROM rubric_grades WHERE
attempt_id = ?').all(attemptId);
+1036|    const rubricScore = rubricGrades.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.score, 0);
+1037|    db.prepare('UPDATE attempts SET status = ?, rubric_score = ? WHERE id 
= ?').run('graded', rubricScore, attemptId);
+1038|  }

+1105|// Outcomes
+1106|app.get('/api/courses/:courseId/outcomes', (req, res) => {
+1107|  const { courseId } = req.params;
+1108|  
+1109|  const course = db.prepare('SELECT instructor_id FROM courses WHERE id = 
?').get(courseId);
+1110|  
+1111|  if (req.user.role === 'student') {
+1112|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Students cannot view full

+1113|  }
+1114|  
+1115|  if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant') {
+1116|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'TAs cannot view full outcomes' 
});
+1117|  }
+1118|  
+1119|  if (course.instructor_id !== req.user.id) {
+1120|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Not authorized' });
+1121|  }
+1122|  
+1123|  const weights = db.prepare('SELECT assessment_id, weight FROM 
outcome_weights WHERE course_id = ? ORDER BY assessment_id').all(courseId);
+1124|  
+1125|  const students = db.prepare(`
+1126|    SELECT DISTINCT u.id, u.name, u.email
+1127|    FROM users u
+1128|    JOIN enrollments e ON u.id = e.user_id
+1129|    WHERE e.course_id = ? AND e.kind = 'student'
+1130|    ORDER BY u.name
+1131|  `).all(courseId);
+1132|  
+1133|  const outcomes = students.map(student => {
+1134|    const row = { studentId: student.id, name: student.name, email:

+1135|

+1137|      const attempt = db.prepare(`

+1139|        FROM attempts a
+1140|        WHERE a.assessment_id = ? AND a.student_id = ?
+1141|        ORDER BY a.started_at DESC
+1142|        LIMIT 1
+1143|      `).get(weight.assessment_id, student.id);
+1144|      
+1145|      const exception = db.prepare(`
+1146|        SELECT excused FROM outcome_exceptions
+1147|        WHERE course_id = ? AND student_id = ? AND assessment_id = ?
+1148|      `).get(courseId, student.id, weight.assessment_id);
+1149|      
+1150|      const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT title FROM assessments WHERE 
id = ?').get(weight.assessment_id);
+1151|      
+1152|      row.assessments[weight.assessment_id] = {
+1153|        title: assessment.title,
+1154|        weight: weight.weight,
+1155|        status: attempt ? attempt.status : 'missing',
+1156|        feedbackStatus: attempt ? attempt.feedback_status : null,
+1157|        objectiveScore: attempt ? attempt.objective_score : null,
+1158|        rubricScore: attempt ? attempt.rubric_score : null,
+1159|        excused: exception ? exception.excused === 1 : false
+1160|      };
+1161|    }
+1162|    
+1163|    return row;
+1164|  });
+1165|  
+1166|  res.json({ weights, outcomes });

+1194|// Worksheet grading
+1195|app.post('/api/attempts/:attemptId/grade-worksheet', (req, res) => {
+1196|  const { attemptId } = req.params;
+1197|  const { operationId, expectedRevision, grades } = req.body;
+1198|  
+1199|  if (req.user.role === 'student') {
+1200|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Cannot grade' });
+1201|  }
+1202|  
+1203|  // Check receipt
+1204|  const receipt = getReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade-worksheet`, req.body);
+1205|  if (receipt) {
+1206|    return 
res.status(receipt.status).json(JSON.parse(receipt.response_json));
+1207|  }
+1208|  
+1209|  const attempt = db.prepare('SELECT assessment_id, status, 
assigned_grader_id FROM attempts WHERE id = ?').get(attemptId);
+1210|  
+1211|  if (!attempt) {
+1212|    const response = { error: 'Attempt not found' };
+1213|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade-worksheet`, req.body, 404, response);
+1214|    return res.status(404).json(response);
+1215|  }
+1216|  
+1217|  if (attempt.status !== 'submitted' && attempt.status !== 'graded') {
+1218|    const response = { error: 'Attempt not gradeable' };
+1219|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade-worksheet`, req.body, 400, response);
+1220|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+1221|  }
+1222|  
+1223|  if (req.user.role === 'teaching_assistant' && attempt.assigned_grader_id
!== req.user.id) {
+1224|    const response = { error: 'Not assigned' };
+1225|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade-worksheet`, req.body, 403, response);
+1226|    return res.status(403).json(response);
+1227|  }
+1228|  
+1229|  // Validate all grades
+1230|  const items = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM items WHERE assessment_id = 
?').all(attempt.assessment_id);
+1231|  
+1232|  for (const grade of grades) {
+1233|    if (!Number.isFinite(grade.score) || grade.score < 0) {
+1234|      const response = { error: 'Invalid score' };
+1235|      memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade-worksheet`, req.body, 400, response);
+1236|      return res.status(400).json(response);
+1237|    }
+1238|    
+1239|    let maxPoints = 0;
+1240|    let foundCriterion = false;
+1241|    
+1242|    for (const item of items) {
+1243|      const rubric = db.prepare('SELECT criteria_json FROM rubrics WHERE 
item_id = ?').get(item.id);
+1244|      if (rubric) {
+1245|        const criteria = JSON.parse(rubric.criteria_json);
+1246|        for (const criterion of criteria) {
+1247|          if (criterion.id === grade.criterionId) {
+1248|            maxPoints = criterion.max_points;
+1249|            foundCriterion = true;
+1250|            break;
+1251|          }
+1252|        }
+1253|      }
+1254|      if (foundCriterion) break;
+1255|    }
+1256|    
+1257|    if (!foundCriterion || grade.score > maxPoints) {
+1258|      const response = { error: 'Invalid criterion or score out of range' 
};
+1259|      memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade-worksheet`, req.body, 400, response);
+1260|      return res.status(400).json(response);
+1261|    }
+1262|  }
+1263|  
+1264|  // Save all grades
+1265|  for (const grade of grades) {
+1266|    db.prepare(`
+1267|      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO rubric_grades (attempt_id, criterion_id, 
score, feedback, graded_by, graded_at)
+1268|      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1269|    `).run(attemptId, grade.criterionId, grade.score, grade.feedback, 
req.user.id, REFERENCE_MOMENT.toISOString());
+1270|  }
+1271|  
+1272|  // Check if all rubric criteria are graded
+1273|  let allGraded = true;
+1274|  for (const item of items) {
+1275|    if (item.kind === 'written') {
+1276|      const rubric = db.prepare('SELECT criteria_json FROM rubrics WHERE 
item_id = ?').get(item.id);
+1277|      if (rubric) {
+1278|        const criteria = JSON.parse(rubric.criteria_json);
+1279|        for (const criterion of criteria) {
+1280|          const gradeRecord = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM rubric_grades 
WHERE attempt_id = ? AND criterion_id = ?').get(attemptId, criterion.id);
+1281|          if (!gradeRecord) {
+1282|            allGraded = false;
+1283|            break;
+1284|          }
+1285|        }
+1286|      }
+1287|    }
+1288|    if (!allGraded) break;
+1289|  }
+1290|  
+1291|  if (allGraded) {
+1292|    const rubricGrades = db.prepare('SELECT score FROM rubric_grades WHERE
attempt_id = ?').all(attemptId);
+1293|    const rubricScore = rubricGrades.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.score, 0);
+1294|    db.prepare('UPDATE attempts SET status = ?, rubric_score = ? WHERE id 
= ?').run('graded', rubricScore, attemptId);
+1295|  }
+1296|  
+1297|  const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT course_id FROM assessments WHERE 
id = ?').get(attempt.assessment_id);
+1298|  
+1299|  const newRevision = incrementCourseRevision(assessment.course_id);
+1300|  logAudit(assessment.course_id, req.user.id, 'grade', 'attempt', 
attemptId, { grades });
+1301|  
+1302|  const response = { id: attemptId, graded: true, courseRevision: 
newRevision };
+1303|  memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/grade-worksheet`, req.body, 200, response);
+1304|  res.json(response);
+1305|});

+1307|// Batch release preview
+1308|app.post('/api/courses/:courseId/release-preview', (req, res) => {
+1309|  const { courseId } = req.params;
+1310|  const { attemptIds } = req.body;
+1311|  
+1312|  if (req.user.role !== 'instructor') {
+1313|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Instructor only' });
+1314|  }
+1315|  
+1316|  const course = db.prepare('SELECT instructor_id, revision FROM courses 
WHERE id = ?').get(courseId);
+1317|  
+1318|  if (course.instructor_id !== req.user.id) {
+1319|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Not authorized' });
+1320|  }
+1321|  
+1322|  if (!Array.isArray(attemptIds) || attemptIds.length === 0) {
+1323|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Empty selection' });
+1324|  }
+1325|  
+1326|  if (new Set(attemptIds).size !== attemptIds.length) {
+1327|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Duplicate attempts' });
+1328|  }
+1329|  
+1330|  const preview = [];
+1331|  const errors = [];
+1332|  
+1333|  for (const attemptId of attemptIds) {
+1334|    const attempt = db.prepare('SELECT assessment_id, student_id, status, 
feedback_status, objective_score, rubric_score FROM attempts WHERE id = 
?').get(attemptId);
+1335|    
+1336|    if (!attempt) {
+1337|      errors.push(`Attempt ${attemptId} not found`);
+1338|      continue;
+1339|    }
+1340|    
+1341|    if (attempt.feedback_status === 'released') {
+1342|      errors.push(`Attempt ${attemptId} already released`);
+1343|      continue;
+1344|    }
+1345|    
+1346|    if (attempt.status !== 'graded') {
+1347|      errors.push(`Attempt ${attemptId} not graded`);
+1348|      continue;
+1349|    }
+1350|    
+1351|    const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT title FROM assessments WHERE id 
= ?').get(attempt.assessment_id);
+1352|    const student = db.prepare('SELECT name, email FROM users WHERE id = 
?').get(attempt.student_id);
+1353|    
+1354|    const total = (attempt.objective_score || 0) + (attempt.rubric_score 
|| 0);
+1355|    
+1356|    preview.push({
+1357|      attemptId,
+1358|      studentId: attempt.student_id,
+1359|      studentName: student.name,
+1360|      studentEmail: student.email,
+1361|      assessmentTitle: assessment.title,
+1362|      total
+1363|    });
+1364|  }
+1365|  
+1366|  if (errors.length > 0) {
+1367|    return res.status(400).json({ errors, validAttempts: preview });
+1368|  }
+1369|  
+1370|  const previewId = 'PREV-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 
9).toUpperCase();
+1371|  db.prepare(`
+1372|    INSERT INTO release_previews (id, course_id, instructor_id, 
course_revision, attempt_ids, consumed, created_at)
+1373|    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1374|  `).run(previewId, courseId, req.user.id, course.revision, 
JSON.stringify(attemptIds), 0, REFERENCE_MOMENT.toISOString());
+1375|  
+1376|  res.json({ previewId, preview });
+1377|});

+1433|  }
+1434|

+1438|

+1444|// Outcome weights and exceptions
+1445|app.post('/api/courses/:courseId/outcome-weights', (req, res) => {
+1446|  const { courseId } = req.params;
+1447|  const { operationId, expectedRevision, weights } = req.body;
+1448|  
+1449|  if (req.user.role !== 'instructor') {
+1450|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Instructor only' });
+1451|  }
+1452|  
+1453|  // Check receipt
+1454|  const receipt = getReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-weights`, req.body);
+1455|  if (receipt) {
+1456|    return 
res.status(receipt.status).json(JSON.parse(receipt.response_json));
+1457|  }
+1458|  
+1459|  const course = db.prepare('SELECT instructor_id, revision FROM courses 
WHERE id = ?').get(courseId);
+1460|  
+1461|  if (course.instructor_id !== req.user.id) {
+1462|    const response = { error: 'Not authorized' };
+1463|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-weights`, req.body, 403, response);
+1464|    return res.status(403).json(response);
+1465|  }
+1466|  
+1467|  if (course.revision !== expectedRevision) {
+1468|    const response = { error: 'Stale revision', currentRevision: 
course.revision };
+1469|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-weights`, req.body, 409, response);
+1470|    return res.status(409).json(response);
+1471|  }
+1472|  
+1473|  // Validate weights
+1474|  const errors = [];
+1475|  let total = 0;
+1476|  const seen = new Set();
+1477|  
+1478|  if (!Array.isArray(weights)) {
+1479|    errors.push('Weights must be an array');
+1480|  } else {
+1481|    for (const w of weights) {
+1482|      if (!w.assessmentId || typeof w.assessmentId !== 'string') {
+1483|        errors.push('Assessment ID required');
+1484|        continue;
+1485|      }
+1486|      
+1487|      if (seen.has(w.assessmentId)) {
+1488|        errors.push('Duplicate assessments');
+1489|        continue;
+1490|      }
+1491|      seen.add(w.assessmentId);
+1492|      
+1493|      if (!Number.isFinite(w.weight) || w.weight < 0) {
+1494|        errors.push('Non-negative numeric weight required');
+1495|        continue;
+1496|      }
+1497|      
+1498|      total += w.weight;
+1499|      
+1500|      const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT status FROM assessments WHERE 
id = ? AND course_id = ?').get(w.assessmentId, courseId);
+1501|      if (!assessment) {
+1502|        errors.push(`Assessment ${w.assessmentId} not found`);
+1503|      } else if (assessment.status !== 'published') {
+1504|        errors.push(`Assessment ${w.assessmentId} not published`);
+1505|      }
+1506|    }
+1507|  }
+1508|  
+1509|  if (Math.abs(total - 1.0) > 0.01) {
+1510|    errors.push('Weights must total 100%');
+1511|  }
+1512|  
+1513|  if (errors.length > 0) {
+1514|    const response = { errors };
+1515|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-weights`, req.body, 400, response);
+1516|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+1517|  }
+1518|  
+1519|  db.prepare('DELETE FROM outcome_weights WHERE course_id = 
?').run(courseId);
+1520|  
+1521|  for (const w of weights) {
+1522|    db.prepare('INSERT INTO outcome_weights (course_id, assessment_id, 
weight) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run(courseId, w.assessmentId, w.weight);
+1523|  }
+1524|  
+1525|  const newRevision = incrementCourseRevision(courseId);
+1526|  logAudit(courseId, req.user.id, 'update_weights', 'course', courseId, { 
weights });
+1527|  
+1528|  const response = { success: true, courseRevision: newRevision };
+1529|  memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-weights`, req.body, 200, response);
+1530|  res.json(response);
+1531|});
+1532|
+1533|app.post('/api/courses/:courseId/outcome-exception', (req, res) => {
+1534|  const { courseId } = req.params;
+1535|  const { operationId, expectedRevision, studentId, assessmentId, excused,
reason } = req.body;
+1536|  
+1537|  if (req.user.role !== 'instructor') {
+1538|    return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Instructor only' });
+1539|  }
+1540|  
+1541|  // Check receipt
+1542|  const receipt = getReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-exception`, req.body);
+1543|  if (receipt) {
+1544|    return 
res.status(receipt.status).json(JSON.parse(receipt.response_json));
+1545|  }
+1546|  
+1547|  const course = db.prepare('SELECT instructor_id, revision FROM courses 
WHERE id = ?').get(courseId);
+1548|  
+1549|  if (course.instructor_id !== req.user.id) {
+1550|    const response = { error: 'Not authorized' };
+1551|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-exception`, req.body, 403, response);
+1552|    return res.status(403).json(response);
+1553|  }
+1554|  
+1555|  if (course.revision !== expectedRevision) {
+1556|    const response = { error: 'Stale revision', currentRevision: 
course.revision };
+1557|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-exception`, req.body, 409, response);
+1558|    return res.status(409).json(response);
+1559|  }
+1560|  
+1561|  const student = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE id = 
?').get(studentId);
+1562|  if (!student) {
+1563|    const response = { error: 'Student not found' };
+1564|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-exception`, req.body, 404, response);
+1565|    return res.status(404).json(response);
+1566|  }
+1567|  
+1568|  const assessment = db.prepare('SELECT status FROM assessments WHERE id =
? AND course_id = ?').get(assessmentId, courseId);
+1569|  if (!assessment || assessment.status !== 'published') {
+1570|    const response = { error: 'Assessment not published' };
+1571|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-exception`, req.body, 400, response);
+1572|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+1573|  }
+1574|  
+1575|  if (excused && !reason) {
+1576|    const response = { error: 'Reason required for excuse' };
+1577|    memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-exception`, req.body, 400, response);
+1578|    return res.status(400).json(response);
+1579|  }
+1580|  
+1581|  if (excused) {
+1582|    db.prepare('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO outcome_exceptions (course_id, 
student_id, assessment_id, excused, reason) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 
?)').run(courseId, studentId, assessmentId, 1, reason);
+1583|  } else {
+1584|    db.prepare('DELETE FROM outcome_exceptions WHERE course_id = ? AND 
student_id = ? AND assessment_id = ?').run(courseId, studentId, assessmentId);
+1585|  }
+1586|  
+1587|  const newRevision = incrementCourseRevision(courseId);
+1588|  logAudit(courseId, req.user.id, excused ? 'excuse' : 'restore', 
'outcome_exception', `${studentId}-${assessmentId}`, { excused, reason });
+1589|  
+1590|  const response = { success: true, courseRevision: newRevision };
+1591|  memoizeReceipt(req.user, operationId, 'POST', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcome-exception`, req.body, 200, response);
+1592|  res.json(response);
+1593|});

Attempts</label>\n          <input type=\"number\" id=\"draftAttempts\" 
min=\"1\" required>\n        </div>\n        <div id=\"draftErrors\"></div>\n   
<button type=\"submit\" style=\"background: var(--primary); color: white; 
border: none; width: 100%;\">Create</button>\n      </form>\n    </div>\n  
</div>\n\n  <div id=\"assessmentDetailModal\" class=\"modal\">\n    <div

<div id=\"attemptModalContent\"></div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n  <div 
class=\"sync-status\" id=\"syncStatus\"></div>\n\n  <script>\n    // Global 
state\n    const app = {\n      token: localStorage.getItem('token'),\n      
user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}'),\n      currentCourse: 
null,\n      currentAssessment: null,\n      currentAttempt: null,\n      
currentView: 'courses',\n      pendingOperations: new Map(),\n      
courseRevisions: new Map(),\n      referenceMoment: new 
Date('2026-09-02T12:00:00Z'),\n      \n      async init() {\n        if 
(this.token) {\n          this.showSignedInUI();\n          await 
this.loadCourses();\n        } else {\n          this.showSignInUI();\n        
}\n      },\n      \n      showSignInUI() {\n        
document.getElementById('signInView').classList.add('active');\n        
document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(nav => nav.style.display = 
'none');\n        document.querySelector('header').style.display = 'none';\n    
document.querySelectorAll('nav a').forEach(a => a.style.display = 'none');\n    
},\n      \n      showSignedInUI() {\n        
document.getElementById('signInView').classList.remove('active');\n        
document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(nav => nav.style.display = 
'inline-block');\n        document.querySelector('header').style.display = 
'flex';\n        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = 
this.user.name || '';\n        document.getElementById('userEmail').textContent 
= this.user.email || '';\n      },\n      \n      async signIn(event) {\n       
event.preventDefault();\n        const email = 
document.getElementById('emailInput').value;\n        const password = 
document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;\n        \n        try {\n      
const response = await fetch('/api/auth/sign-in', {\n            method: 
'POST',\n            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n         
body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })\n          });\n          \n          
if (!response.ok) {\n            const data = await response.json();\n          
document.getElementById('signInError').textContent = data.error || 'Sign in 
failed';\n            return;\n          }\n          \n          const data = 
await response.json();\n          this.token = data.token;\n          this.user 
= data.user;\n          localStorage.setItem('token', this.token);\n          
localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(this.user));\n          \n          
this.showSignedInUI();\n          await this.loadCourses();\n          
this.switchView('courses');\n        } catch (error) {\n          
document.getElementById('signInError').textContent = 'Network error: ' + 
error.message;\n        }\n      },\n      \n      async signOut() {\n        
try {\n          await fetch('/api/auth/sign-out', {\n            method: 
'POST',\n            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }\n     
});\n        } catch (error) {\n          console.error('Sign out error:', 
error);\n        }\n        \n        this.token = null;\n        this.user = 
{};\n        localStorage.removeItem('token');\n        
localStorage.removeItem('user');\n        location.reload();\n      },\n      \n
async api(method, path, body = null) {\n        const opts = {\n          
method,\n          headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }\n       
};\n        \n        if (body) {\n          opts.headers['Content-Type'] = 
'application/json';\n          opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);\n        }\n   
\n        const response = await fetch(path, opts);\n        if (response.status
=== 401) {\n          this.showSignInUI();\n          throw new Error('Not 
authenticated');\n        }\n        \n        const data = await 
response.json();\n        return { status: response.status, data };\n      },\n 
\n      generateOperationId() {\n        return crypto.getRandomValues(new 
Uint8Array(16)).reduce((s, b) => s + b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'), '');\n    
},\n      \n      async loadCourses() {\n        try {\n          const { data }
= await this.api('GET', '/api/courses');\n          const html = data.length ===
0 \n            ? '<div class=\"empty-state\"><p>No courses 
enrolled</p></div>'\n            : data.map(c => `\n              <div 
class=\"card\">\n                <h3 class=\"card-title\">${c.title}</h3>\n     
<p style=\"color: var(--gray); font-size: 0.875rem; margin: 0.5rem 0 0 
0;\">${c.id}</p>\n              </div>\n            `).join('');\n          \n  
document.getElementById('coursesList').innerHTML = html;\n          \n          
// Populate course selects\n          const selects = ['courseSelect', 
'courseSelect2', 'courseSelect3', 'courseSelect4'];\n          for (const 
selectId of selects) {\n            const select = 
document.getElementById(selectId);\n            select.innerHTML = '<option 
value=\"\">Choose a course...</option>';\n            for (const course of data)
{\n              select.innerHTML += `<option 
value=\"${course.id}\">${course.title}</option>`;\n            }\n          }\n 
} catch (error) {\n          console.error('Load courses error:', error);\n     
}\n      },\n      \n      switchView(view) {\n        this.currentView = 
view;\n        document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v => 
v.classList.remove('active'));\n        document.getElementById(view + 
'View').classList.add('active');\n        document.querySelectorAll('nav 
a').forEach(a => a.removeAttribute('aria-current'));\n        
document.querySelector(`nav 
a[data-view=\"${view}\"]`).setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');\n      },\n    
\n      async loadAssessments() {\n        const courseId = 
document.getElementById('courseSelect').value;\n        if (!courseId) return;\n
\n        this.currentCourse = courseId;\n        \n        try {\n          
const { data } = await this.api('GET', `/api/assessments/${courseId}`);\n       
\n          let html = '';\n          if (data.length === 0) {\n            html
= '<div class=\"empty-state\"><p>No assessments</p></div>';\n          } else 
{\n            html = data.map(a => `\n              <div class=\"card\">\n     
<div class=\"card-header\">\n                  <h3 
class=\"card-title\">${a.title}</h3>\n                  <span 
class=\"status-badge status-${a.status}\">${a.status}</span>\n                
</div>\n                <p><strong>Opens:</strong> ${new 
Date(a.opens_at).toLocaleString()}</p>\n                <p><strong>Due:</strong>
${new Date(a.due_at).toLocaleString()}</p>\n                
<p><strong>Duration:</strong> ${a.duration_minutes} minutes | <strong>Max 
Attempts:</strong> ${a.max_attempts}</p>\n                <div 
class=\"action-group\">\n                  <button 
onclick=\"app.viewAssessment('${courseId}', '${a.id}')\">View Details</button>\n
${this.user.role === 'instructor' && a.status === 'draft' \n                    
? `<button onclick=\"app.showEditDraftModal('${courseId}', 
'${a.id}')\">Edit</button>` \n                    : ''}\n                  
${this.user.role === 'student' && a.status === 'published'\n                    
? `<button onclick=\"app.startAttempt('${courseId}', '${a.id}')\">Start 
Attempt</button>`\n                    : ''}\n                </div>\n          
</div>\n            `).join('');\n          }\n          \n          
document.getElementById('assessmentsList').innerHTML = html;\n          
document.getElementById('instructorActions').style.display = this.user.role === 
'instructor' ? 'block' : 'none';\n        } catch (error) {\n          
console.error('Load assessments error:', error);\n        }\n      },\n      \n 
async viewAssessment(courseId, assessmentId) {\n        try {\n          const {
data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}`);\n          \n          let 
itemsHtml = '';\n          for (const item of data.items) {\n            
itemsHtml += `\n              <div class=\"question-card\">\n                
<div class=\"question-prompt\">${item.prompt}</div>\n                ${item.kind
=== 'multiple_choice' \n                  ? `<div style=\"font-size: 0.875rem; 
color: var(--gray);\">Multiple Choice (${item.points} points)</div>` +\n        
item.options.map(o => `<div class=\"question-answer\">${o}</div>`).join('')\n   
: `<div style=\"font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--gray);\">Written Response 
(${item.points} points)</div>`}\n                ${this.user.role !== 'student' 
&& item.kind === 'multiple_choice'\n                  ? `<div 
style=\"background: #fee2e2; padding: 0.5rem; border-radius: 0.25rem; 
margin-top: 0.5rem; font-size: 0.875rem;\"><strong>Answer Key:</strong> 
${item.answer}</div>`\n                  : ''}\n              </div>\n          
`;\n          }\n          \n          
document.getElementById('assessmentDetailTitle').textContent = data.title;\n    
document.getElementById('assessmentDetailContent').innerHTML = itemsHtml;\n     
this.openModal('assessmentDetailModal');\n        } catch (error) {\n          
console.error('View assessment error:', error);\n        }\n      },\n      \n  
showCreateDraftModal() {\n        document.getElementById('draftTitle').value = 
'';\n        document.getElementById('draftOpens').value = '';\n        
document.getElementById('draftDue').value = '';\n        
document.getElementById('draftDuration').value = '';\n        
document.getElementById('draftAttempts').value = '';\n        
document.getElementById('draftErrors').innerHTML = '';\n        
this.openModal('createDraftModal');\n      },\n      \n      async 
createDraft(event) {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        const courseId = 
document.getElementById('courseSelect').value;\n        if (!courseId) {\n      
alert('Select a course first');\n          return;\n        }\n        \n       
const title = document.getElementById('draftTitle').value;\n        const 
opens_at = new 
Date(document.getElementById('draftOpens').value).toISOString();\n        const 
due_at = new Date(document.getElementById('draftDue').value).toISOString();\n   
const duration_minutes = 
parseInt(document.getElementById('draftDuration').value);\n        const 
max_attempts = parseInt(document.getElementById('draftAttempts').value);\n      
\n        const operationId = this.generateOperationId();\n        const body = 
{ operationId, title, opens_at, due_at, duration_minutes, max_attempts };\n     
\n        try {\n          const { status, data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/create-draft`, body);\n          \n          if 
(status === 201) {\n            this.closeModal('createDraftModal');\n          
this.showStatus('Draft created successfully', 'success');\n            await 
this.loadAssessments();\n          } else {\n            const errors = 
data.errors || [data.error];\n            
document.getElementById('draftErrors').innerHTML = errors.map(e => `<div 
class=\"error-message\">${e}</div>`).join('');\n          }\n        } catch 
(error) {\n          console.error('Create draft error:', error);\n        }\n  
},\n      \n      async loadAttempts() {\n        const courseId = 
document.getElementById('courseSelect2').value;\n        if (!courseId) 
return;\n        \n        try {\n          const { data: assessments } = await 
this.api('GET', `/api/assessments/${courseId}`);\n          \n          let html
= '';\n          for (const assessment of assessments) {\n            const { 
data: details } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessment.id}`);\n            if 
(details.items) {\n              html += `\n                <div 
class=\"card\">\n                  <h3 
class=\"card-title\">${assessment.title}</h3>\n                  <div 
class=\"action-group\">\n                    <button 
onclick=\"app.viewAttemptForAssessment('${courseId}', '${assessment.id}')\">View
Attempts</button>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n           
`;\n            }\n          }\n          \n          if (!html) {\n            
html = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><p>No assessments available</p></div>';\n    
}\n          \n          document.getElementById('attemptsList').innerHTML = 
html;\n        } catch (error) {\n          console.error('Load attempts 
error:', error);\n        }\n      },\n      \n      async 
viewAttemptForAssessment(courseId, assessmentId) {\n        try {\n          
const { data: assessment } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}`);\n          \n          let html
= '<div>';\n          \n          if (this.user.role === 'student') {\n         
const allAttempts = [];\n            let page = 0;\n            \n            
const attemptList = localStorage.getItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`) || '[]';\n 
const stored = JSON.parse(attemptList);\n            \n            for (const 
attempt of stored) {\n              try {\n                const { data } = 
await this.api('GET', `/api/attempts/${attempt.id}`);\n                
allAttempts.push(data);\n              } catch (error) {\n                // 
Skip this attempt\n              }\n            }\n            \n            if 
(allAttempts.length === 0) {\n              html += '<p>No attempts yet</p>';\n 
} else {\n              html += 
'<table><thead><tr><th>Started</th><th>Status</th><th>Score</th><th>Action</th><
/tr></thead><tbody>';\n              for (const attempt of allAttempts) {\n     
const score = attempt.status === 'in_progress' ? 'In progress' : \n             
attempt.feedbackStatus === 'released' ? `${(attempt.objectiveScore || 0) + 
(attempt.rubricScore || 0)}` : 'Not released';\n                html += 
`<tr><td>${new Date(attempt.startedAt).toLocaleString()}</td><td><span 
class=\"status-badge 
status-${attempt.status}\">${attempt.status}</span></td><td>${score}</td><td><bu
tton onclick=\"app.resumeAttempt('${attempt.id}')\">View</button></td></tr>`;\n 
}\n              html += '</tbody></table>';\n            }\n          }\n      
\n          html += '</div>';\n          \n          
document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = `${assessment.title} 
- Attempts`;\n          document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML
= html;\n          this.openModal('attemptModal');\n        } catch (error) {\n 
console.error('View attempt error:', error);\n        }\n      },\n      \n     
async startAttempt(courseId, assessmentId) {\n        const operationId = 
this.generateOperationId();\n        const body = { operationId, assessmentId, 
expectedRevision: this.courseRevisions.get(courseId) || 0 };\n        \n        
try {\n          const { status, data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${courseId}/start`, body);\n          \n          if (status === 
201) {\n            const attempts = 
JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`) || '[]');\n         
attempts.push({ id: data.id });\n            
localStorage.setItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`, JSON.stringify(attempts));\n   
\n            await this.resumeAttempt(data.id);\n          } else {\n          
alert(data.error || 'Failed to start attempt');\n          }\n        } catch 
(error) {\n          console.error('Start attempt error:', error);\n        }\n 
},\n      \n      async resumeAttempt(attemptId) {\n        try {\n          
const { data } = await this.api('GET', `/api/attempts/${attemptId}`);\n         
this.currentAttempt = data;\n          \n          if (this.user.role === 
'student' && data.status === 'in_progress') {\n            await 
this.showAttemptInterface(attemptId, data);\n          } else {\n            
await this.showAttemptSummary(attemptId, data);\n          }\n        } catch 
(error) {\n          console.error('Resume attempt error:', error);\n        }\n
},\n      \n      async showAttemptInterface(attemptId, attempt) {\n        try 
{\n          const { data: assessment } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${attempt.assessmentId}`);\n          \n          let html = 
'<div>';\n          \n          // Timer\n          const startTime = new 
Date(attempt.startedAt);\n          const now = this.referenceMoment;\n         
const elapsed = (now - startTime) / 1000 / 60;\n          const remaining = 
assessment.duration_minutes - elapsed;\n          \n          html += `<div 
style=\"background: var(--bg-light); padding: 1rem; border-radius: 0.375rem; 
margin-bottom: 1rem;\">`;\n          html += `<p style=\"margin: 0; font-size: 
0.875rem; color: var(--gray);\">Time Remaining</p>`;\n          html += `<p 
class=\"countdown\" style=\"margin: 0;\">${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(remaining))} 
minutes</p>`;\n          html += `</div>`;\n          \n          // Questions\n
for (const item of assessment.items) {\n            const answer = 
attempt.answers ? attempt.answers[item.id] : null;\n            \n            
html += `<div class=\"question-card\">`;\n            html += `<div 
class=\"question-prompt\">${item.prompt}</div>`;\n            \n            if 
(item.kind === 'multiple_choice') {\n              html += `<div>`;\n           
for (const option of item.options) {\n                const checked = answer ===
option ? 'checked' : '';\n                html += `<label style=\"display: 
block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;\"><input type=\"radio\" name=\"item_${item.id}\" 
value=\"${option}\" ${checked} onchange=\"app.saveAnswer('${attemptId}', 
'${item.id}', this.value)\"> ${option}</label>`;\n              }\n             
html += `</div>`;\n            } else {\n              html += `<textarea 
name=\"item_${item.id}\" style=\"min-height: 100px;\" 
onchange=\"app.saveAnswer('${attemptId}', '${item.id}', this.value)\">${answer 
|| ''}</textarea>`;\n            }\n            \n            html += 
`</div>`;\n          }\n          \n          html += `<div 
class=\"action-group\">`;\n          html += `<button 
onclick=\"app.submitAttempt('${attemptId}')\" style=\"background: 
var(--success); color: white; border: none;\">Submit Attempt</button>`;\n       
html += `</div>`;\n          html += `</div>`;\n          \n          
document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
attempt.assessmentTitle;\n          
document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;\n          
this.openModal('attemptModal');\n        } catch (error) {\n          
console.error('Show attempt interface error:', error);\n        }\n      },\n   
\n      async saveAnswer(attemptId, itemId, value) {\n        const operationId 
= this.generateOperationId();\n        const body = { operationId, itemId, value
};\n        \n        try {\n          await this.api('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/save-answer`, body);\n          
this.currentAttempt.answers = this.currentAttempt.answers || {};\n          
this.currentAttempt.answers[itemId] = value;\n        } catch (error) {\n       
console.error('Save answer error:', error);\n        }\n      },\n      \n      
async submitAttempt(attemptId) {\n        if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to
submit your attempt?')) return;\n        \n        const operationId = 
this.generateOperationId();\n        const body = { operationId };\n        \n  
try {\n          const { data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/submit`, body);\n          this.showStatus('Attempt 
submitted successfully', 'success');\n          
this.closeModal('attemptModal');\n          await this.loadAttempts();\n        
} catch (error) {\n          console.error('Submit attempt error:', error);\n   
}\n      },\n      \n      async showAttemptSummary(attemptId, attempt) {\n     
let html = '<div>';\n        \n        html += `<p><strong>Status:</strong> 
${attempt.status}</p>`;\n        html += `<p><strong>Started:</strong> ${new 
Date(attempt.startedAt).toLocaleString()}</p>`;\n        \n        if 
(attempt.submittedAt) {\n          html += `<p><strong>Submitted:</strong> ${new
Date(attempt.submittedAt).toLocaleString()}</p>`;\n        }\n        \n        
if (attempt.feedbackStatus === 'released') {\n          html += 
`<p><strong>Score:</strong> ${(attempt.objectiveScore || 0) + 
(attempt.rubricScore || 0)}</p>`;\n          if (attempt.grades) {\n            
html += '<div style=\"margin-top: 1rem;\"><strong>Rubric Grades:</strong>';\n   
for (const grade of attempt.grades) {\n              html += `<div 
style=\"padding: 0.5rem; background: var(--bg-light); border-radius: 0.25rem; 
margin-top: 0.5rem;\">`;\n              html += 
`<div><strong>${grade.criterion_id}</strong>: ${grade.score} points</div>`;\n   
html += `<div style=\"color: var(--gray); font-size: 
0.875rem;\">${grade.feedback}</div>`;\n              html += `</div>`;\n        
}\n            html += '</div>';\n          }\n        }\n        \n        html
+= '</div>';\n        \n        
document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
attempt.assessmentTitle;\n        
document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;\n        
this.openModal('attemptModal');\n      },\n      \n      async loadGradebook() 
{\n        const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect3').value;\n   
if (!courseId) return;\n        \n        this.currentCourse = courseId;\n      
\n        if (this.user.role === 'student') {\n          
document.getElementById('gradebookTabs').style.display = 'none';\n          // 
Show student's own outcomes\n          try {\n            const { data } = await
this.api('GET', `/api/courses/${courseId}/outcomes`);\n            // Students 
only see their own row\n            // This is handled by the server\n          
} catch (error) {\n            console.error('Load gradebook error:', error);\n 
}\n        } else {\n          
document.getElementById('gradebookTabs').style.display = 'block';\n          
this.switchGradebookTab('outcomes');\n        }\n      },\n      \n      
switchGradebookTab(tab) {\n        
document.getElementById('outcomesTab').style.display = tab === 'outcomes' ? 
'block' : 'none';\n        document.getElementById('worksheetTab').style.display
= tab === 'worksheet' ? 'block' : 'none';\n        
document.getElementById('releaseTab').style.display = tab === 'release' ? 
'block' : 'none';\n        \n        if (tab === 'outcomes') {\n          
this.loadOutcomesLedger();\n        } else if (tab === 'worksheet') {\n         
this.loadGradingWorksheet();\n        } else if (tab === 'release') {\n         
this.loadBatchRelease();\n        }\n      },\n      \n      async 
loadOutcomesLedger() {\n        const courseId = this.currentCourse;\n        if
(!courseId) return;\n        \n        try {\n          const { data } = await 
this.api('GET', `/api/courses/${courseId}/outcomes`);\n          \n          if 
(!data || !data.outcomes) {\n            
document.getElementById('outcomesTab').innerHTML = '<p>No data</p>';\n          
return;\n          }\n          \n          let html = 
'<table><thead><tr><th>Student</th>';\n          for (const weight of 
data.weights) {\n            html += `<th>${weight.assessment_id} 
(${(weight.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%)</th>`;\n          }\n          html += 
'<th>Grade</th></tr></thead><tbody>';\n          \n          for (const student 
of data.outcomes) {\n            html += `<tr><td>${student.name}</td>`;\n      
\n            for (const weight of data.weights) {\n              const assess =
student.assessments[weight.assessment_id];\n              let cellContent = 
'\u2014';\n              \n              if (assess.excused) {\n                
cellContent = 'Excused';\n              } else if (assess.status === 'missing') 
{\n                cellContent = 'Missing';\n              } else if 
(assess.feedbackStatus !== 'released') {\n                cellContent = 
'Pending';\n              } else if (assess.objectiveScore !== null) {\n        
const max = 10; // TODO: calculate from items\n                cellContent = 
`${assess.objectiveScore + (assess.rubricScore || 0)}/${max}`;\n              
}\n              \n              html += `<td>${cellContent}</td>`;\n           
}\n            \n            html += '<td>\u2014</td>';\n            html += 
'</tr>';\n          }\n          \n          html += '</tbody></table>';\n      
document.getElementById('outcomesTab').innerHTML = html;\n        } catch 
(error) {\n          console.error('Load outcomes error:', error);\n        }\n 
},\n      \n      async loadGradingWorksheet() {\n        const courseId = 
this.currentCourse;\n        if (!courseId) return;\n        \n        try {\n  
const { data: assessments } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}`);\n          \n          let html = '<div>';\n   
\n          for (const assessment of assessments) {\n            if 
(assessment.status !== 'published') continue;\n            \n            html +=
`<div class=\"card\">`;\n            html += `<h3 
class=\"card-title\">${assessment.title}</h3>`;\n            html += `<button 
onclick=\"app.selectSubmissionsForGrading('${courseId}', 
'${assessment.id}')\">Grade Submissions</button>`;\n            html += 
`</div>`;\n          }\n          \n          html += '</div>';\n          
document.getElementById('worksheetTab').innerHTML = html;\n        } catch 
(error) {\n          console.error('Load worksheet error:', error);\n        }\n
},\n      \n      async selectSubmissionsForGrading(courseId, assessmentId) {\n 
// This would show a list of submitted attempts and allow worksheet grading\n   
alert('Worksheet grading interface');\n      },\n      \n      async 
loadBatchRelease() {\n        const courseId = this.currentCourse;\n        if 
(!courseId) return;\n        \n        try {\n          const { data: 
assessments } = await this.api('GET', `/api/assessments/${courseId}`);\n        
\n          let html = '<div>';\n          html += '<p>Select attempts to 
release feedback in a batch:</p>';\n          html += '<div 
id=\"releaseSelection\"></div>';\n          html += '<button 
onclick=\"app.previewBatchRelease()\">Preview Release</button>';\n          html
+= '</div>';\n          \n          
document.getElementById('releaseTab').innerHTML = html;\n        } catch (error)
{\n          console.error('Load batch release error:', error);\n        }\n    
},\n      \n      async loadAudit() {\n        const courseId = 
document.getElementById('courseSelect4').value;\n        if (!courseId) 
return;\n        \n        try {\n          const { data } = await 
this.api('GET', `/api/audit/${courseId}`);\n          \n          let html = 
'<table><thead><tr><th>Timestamp</th><th>Event</th><th>Details</th></tr></thead>
<tbody>';\n          \n          for (const event of data) {\n            html 
+= `<tr>`;\n            html += `<td>${new 
Date(event.timestamp).toLocaleString()}</td>`;\n            html += 
`<td>${event.event_type}</td>`;\n            html += 
`<td>${event.details}</td>`;\n            html += `</tr>`;\n          }\n       
\n          html += '</tbody></table>';\n          \n          if (data.length 
=== 0) {\n            html = '<div class=\"empty-state\"><p>No audit 
events</p></div>';\n          }\n          \n          
document.getElementById('auditList').innerHTML = html;\n        } catch (error) 
{\n          console.error('Load audit error:', error);\n        }\n      },\n  
\n      openModal(modalId) {\n        
document.getElementById(modalId).classList.add('open');\n        
document.getElementById(modalId).focus();\n      },\n      \n      
closeModal(modalId) {\n        
document.getElementById(modalId).classList.remove('open');\n      },\n      \n  
showStatus(message, type) {\n        const status = 
document.getElementById('syncStatus');\n        status.textContent = message;\n 
status.className = `sync-status show ${type}`;\n        setTimeout(() => {\n    
status.classList.remove('show');\n        }, 3000);\n      }\n    };\n    \n    
// Initialize on load\n    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => 
{\n      app.init();\n    });\n    \n    // Handle modal close with Escape\n    
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {\n      if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
document.querySelectorAll('.modal.open').forEach(m => {\n          
m.classList.remove('open');\n        });\n      }\n    });\n    \n    // Handle 
reduced motion preference\n    if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: 
reduce)').matches) {\n      
document.body.classList.add('prefers-reduced-motion');\n    }\n  
</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"}

Reasoning:
Now let me create the comprehensive frontend HTML with all the necessary 
functionality. This needs to include:

1. Sign-in view
2. Courses view
3. Assessments view
4. Attempts view (for students taking tests)
5. Gradebook view (for instructors/TAs)
6. Audit view

I'll need to include all the JavaScript logic for handling the various 
workflows, managing sessions, handling retries with operation IDs, managing 
revisions, and handling private data correctly.

Let me create a comprehensive single-page application.
