      <script>
        // Global state
        const app = {
          token: localStorage.getItem('token'),
          user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}'),
          currentCourse: null,
          currentAssessment: null,
          currentAttempt: null,
          currentView: 'courses',
          pendingOperations: new Map(),
          courseRevisions: new Map(),
          referenceMoment: new Date('2026-09-02T12:00:00Z'),
          
          async init() {
            if (this.token) {
              this.showSignedInUI();
              await this.loadCourses();
            } else {
              this.showSignInUI();
            }
          },
          
          showSignInUI() {
            document.getElementById('signInView').classList.add('active');
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(nav => 
nav.style.display = 'none');
            document.querySelector('header').style.display = 'none';
            document.querySelectorAll('nav a').forEach(a => a.style.display = 
'none');
          },
          
          showSignedInUI() {
            document.getElementById('signInView').classList.remove('active');
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(nav => 
nav.style.display = 'inline-block');
            document.querySelector('header').style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('userName').textContent = this.user.name || 
'';
            document.getElementById('userEmail').textContent = this.user.email 
|| '';
          },
          
          async signIn(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const email = document.getElementById('emailInput').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;
            
            try {
              const response = await fetch('/api/auth/sign-in', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
              });
              
              if (!response.ok) {
                const data = await response.json();
                document.getElementById('signInError').textContent = data.error 
|| 'Sign in failed';
                return;
              }
              
              const data = await response.json();
              this.token = data.token;
              this.user = data.user;
              localStorage.setItem('token', this.token);
              localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(this.user));
              
              this.showSignedInUI();
              await this.loadCourses();
              this.switchView('courses');
            } catch (error) {
              document.getElementById('signInError').textContent = 'Network 
error: ' + error.message;
            }
          },
          
          async signOut() {
            try {
              await fetch('/api/auth/sign-out', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
              });
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Sign out error:', error);
            }
            
            this.token = null;
            this.user = {};
            localStorage.removeItem('token');
            localStorage.removeItem('user');
            location.reload();
          },
          
          async api(method, path, body = null) {
            const opts = {
              method,
              headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
            };
            
            if (body) {
              opts.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
              opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
            }
            
            const response = await fetch(path, opts);
            if (response.status === 401) {
              this.showSignInUI();
              throw new Error('Not authenticated');
            }
            
            const data = await response.json();
            return { status: response.status, data };
          },
          
          generateOperationId() {
            return crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)).reduce((s, b) => s
+ b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'), '');
          },
          
          async loadCourses() {
            try {
              const { data } = await this.api('GET', '/api/courses');
              const html = data.length === 0 
                ? '<div class="empty-state"><p>No courses enrolled</p></div>'
                : data.map(c => `
                  <div class="card">
                    <h3 class="card-title">${c.title}</h3>
                    <p style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 0.875rem; margin: 
0.5rem 0 0 0;">${c.id}</p>
                  </div>
                `).join('');
              
              document.getElementById('coursesList').innerHTML = html;
              
              // Populate course selects
              const selects = ['courseSelect', 'courseSelect2', 'courseSelect3',
'courseSelect4'];
              for (const selectId of selects) {
                const select = document.getElementById(selectId);
                select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Choose a 
course...</option>';
                for (const course of data) {
                  select.innerHTML += `<option 
value="${course.id}">${course.title}</option>`;
                }
              }
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Load courses error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          switchView(view) {
            this.currentView = view;
            document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v => 
v.classList.remove('active'));
            document.getElementById(view + 'View').classList.add('active');
            document.querySelectorAll('nav a').forEach(a => 
a.removeAttribute('aria-current'));
            document.querySelector(`nav 
a[data-view="${view}"]`).setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
          },
          
          async loadAssessments() {
            const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect').value;
            if (!courseId) return;
            
            this.currentCourse = courseId;
            
            try {
              const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}`);
              
              let html = '';
              if (data.length === 0) {
                html = '<div class="empty-state"><p>No assessments</p></div>';
              } else {
                html = data.map(a => `
                  <div class="card">
                    <div class="card-header">
                      <h3 class="card-title">${a.title}</h3>
                      <span class="status-badge 
status-${a.status}">${a.status}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p><strong>Opens:</strong> ${new 
Date(a.opens_at).toLocaleString()}</p>
                    <p><strong>Due:</strong> ${new 
Date(a.due_at).toLocaleString()}</p>
                    <p><strong>Duration:</strong> ${a.duration_minutes} minutes 
| <strong>Max Attempts:</strong> ${a.max_attempts}</p>
                    <div class="action-group">
                      <button onclick="app.viewAssessment('${courseId}', 
'${a.id}')">View Details</button>
                      ${this.user.role === 'instructor' && a.status === 'draft' 
                        ? `<button 
onclick="app.showEditDraftModal('${courseId}', '${a.id}')">Edit</button>` 
                        : ''}
                      ${this.user.role === 'student' && a.status === 'published'
                        ? `<button onclick="app.startAttempt('${courseId}', 
'${a.id}')">Start Attempt</button>`
                        : ''}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `).join('');
              }
              
              document.getElementById('assessmentsList').innerHTML = html;
              document.getElementById('instructorActions').style.display = 
this.user.role === 'instructor' ? 'block' : 'none';
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Load assessments error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async viewAssessment(courseId, assessmentId) {
            try {
              const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}`);
              
              let itemsHtml = '';
              for (const item of data.items) {
                itemsHtml += `
                  <div class="question-card">
                    <div class="question-prompt">${item.prompt}</div>
                    ${item.kind === 'multiple_choice' 
                      ? `<div style="font-size: 0.875rem; color: 
var(--gray);">Multiple Choice (${item.points} points)</div>` +
                        item.options.map(o => `<div 
class="question-answer">${o}</div>`).join('')
                      : `<div style="font-size: 0.875rem; color: 
var(--gray);">Written Response (${item.points} points)</div>`}
                    ${this.user.role !== 'student' && item.kind === 
'multiple_choice'
                      ? `<div style="background: #fee2e2; padding: 0.5rem; 
border-radius: 0.25rem; margin-top: 0.5rem; font-size: 0.875rem;"><strong>Answer
Key:</strong> ${item.answer}</div>`
                      : ''}
                  </div>
                `;
              }
              
              document.getElementById('assessmentDetailTitle').textContent = 
data.title;
              document.getElementById('assessmentDetailContent').innerHTML = 
itemsHtml;
              this.openModal('assessmentDetailModal');
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('View assessment error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          showCreateDraftModal() {
            document.getElementById('draftTitle').value = '';
            document.getElementById('draftOpens').value = '';
            document.getElementById('draftDue').value = '';
            document.getElementById('draftDuration').value = '';
            document.getElementById('draftAttempts').value = '';
            document.getElementById('draftErrors').innerHTML = '';
            this.openModal('createDraftModal');
          },
          
          async createDraft(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect').value;
            if (!courseId) {
              alert('Select a course first');
              return;
            }
            
            const title = document.getElementById('draftTitle').value;
            const opens_at = new 
Date(document.getElementById('draftOpens').value).toISOString();
            const due_at = new 
Date(document.getElementById('draftDue').value).toISOString();
            const duration_minutes = 
parseInt(document.getElementById('draftDuration').value);
            const max_attempts = 
parseInt(document.getElementById('draftAttempts').value);
            
            const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
            const body = { operationId, title, opens_at, due_at, 
duration_minutes, max_attempts };
            
            try {
              const { status, data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/create-draft`, body);
              
              if (status === 201) {
                this.closeModal('createDraftModal');
                this.showStatus('Draft created successfully', 'success');
                await this.loadAssessments();
              } else {
                const errors = data.errors || [data.error];
                document.getElementById('draftErrors').innerHTML = errors.map(e 
=> `<div class="error-message">${e}</div>`).join('');
              }
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Create draft error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async loadAttempts() {
            const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect2').value;
            if (!courseId) return;
            
            try {
              const { data: assessments } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}`);
              
              let html = '';
              for (const assessment of assessments) {
                const { data: details } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessment.id}`);
                if (details.items) {
                  html += `
                    <div class="card">
                      <h3 class="card-title">${assessment.title}</h3>
                      <div class="action-group">
                        <button 
onclick="app.viewAttemptForAssessment('${courseId}', '${assessment.id}')">View 
Attempts</button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  `;
                }
              }
              
              if (!html) {
                html = '<div class="empty-state"><p>No assessments 
available</p></div>';
              }
              
              document.getElementById('attemptsList').innerHTML = html;
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Load attempts error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async viewAttemptForAssessment(courseId, assessmentId) {
            try {
              const { data: assessment } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}`);
              
              let html = '<div>';
              
              if (this.user.role === 'student') {
                const allAttempts = [];
                let page = 0;
                
                const attemptList = 
localStorage.getItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`) || '[]';
                const stored = JSON.parse(attemptList);
                
                for (const attempt of stored) {
                  try {
                    const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${attempt.id}`);
                    allAttempts.push(data);
                  } catch (error) {
                    // Skip this attempt
                  }
                }
                
                if (allAttempts.length === 0) {
                  html += '<p>No attempts yet</p>';
                } else {
                  html += 
'<table><thead><tr><th>Started</th><th>Status</th><th>Score</th><th>Action</th><
/tr></thead><tbody>';
                  for (const attempt of allAttempts) {
                    const score = attempt.status === 'in_progress' ? 'In 
progress' : 
                      attempt.feedbackStatus === 'released' ? 
`${(attempt.objectiveScore || 0) + (attempt.rubricScore || 0)}` : 'Not 
released';
                    html += `<tr><td>${new 
Date(attempt.startedAt).toLocaleString()}</td><td><span class="status-badge 
status-${attempt.status}">${attempt.status}</span></td><td>${score}</td><td><but
ton onclick="app.resumeAttempt('${attempt.id}')">View</button></td></tr>`;
                  }
                  html += '</tbody></table>';
                }
              }
              
              html += '</div>';
              
              document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
`${assessment.title} - Attempts`;
              document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;
              this.openModal('attemptModal');
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('View attempt error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async startAttempt(courseId, assessmentId) {
            const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
            const body = { operationId, assessmentId, expectedRevision: 
this.courseRevisions.get(courseId) || 0 };
            
            try {
              const { status, data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${courseId}/start`, body);
              
              if (status === 201) {
                const attempts = 
JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`) || '[]');
                attempts.push({ id: data.id });
                localStorage.setItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`, 
JSON.stringify(attempts));
                
                await this.resumeAttempt(data.id);
              } else {
                alert(data.error || 'Failed to start attempt');
              }
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Start attempt error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async resumeAttempt(attemptId) {
            try {
              const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}`);
              this.currentAttempt = data;
              
              if (this.user.role === 'student' && data.status === 'in_progress')
{
                await this.showAttemptInterface(attemptId, data);
              } else {
                await this.showAttemptSummary(attemptId, data);
              }
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Resume attempt error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async showAttemptInterface(attemptId, attempt) {
            try {
              const { data: assessment } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${attempt.assessmentId}`);
              
              let html = '<div>';
              
              // Timer
              const startTime = new Date(attempt.startedAt);
              const now = this.referenceMoment;
              const elapsed = (now - startTime) / 1000 / 60;
              const remaining = assessment.duration_minutes - elapsed;
              
              html += `<div style="background: var(--bg-light); padding: 1rem; 
border-radius: 0.375rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">`;
              html += `<p style="margin: 0; font-size: 0.875rem; color: 
var(--gray);">Time Remaining</p>`;
              html += `<p class="countdown" style="margin: 0;">${Math.max(0, 
Math.ceil(remaining))} minutes</p>`;
              html += `</div>`;
              
              // Questions
              for (const item of assessment.items) {
                const answer = attempt.answers ? attempt.answers[item.id] : 
null;
                
                html += `<div class="question-card">`;
                html += `<div class="question-prompt">${item.prompt}</div>`;
                
                if (item.kind === 'multiple_choice') {
                  html += `<div>`;
                  for (const option of item.options) {
                    const checked = answer === option ? 'checked' : '';
                    html += `<label style="display: block; margin-bottom: 
0.5rem;"><input type="radio" name="item_${item.id}" value="${option}" ${checked}
onchange="app.saveAnswer('${attemptId}', '${item.id}', this.value)"> 
${option}</label>`;
                  }
                  html += `</div>`;
                } else {
                  html += `<textarea name="item_${item.id}" style="min-height: 
100px;" onchange="app.saveAnswer('${attemptId}', '${item.id}', 
this.value)">${answer || ''}</textarea>`;
                }
                
                html += `</div>`;
              }
              
              html += `<div class="action-group">`;
              html += `<button onclick="app.submitAttempt('${attemptId}')" 
style="background: var(--success); color: white; border: none;">Submit 
Attempt</button>`;
              html += `</div>`;
              html += `</div>`;
              
              document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
attempt.assessmentTitle;
              document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;
              this.openModal('attemptModal');
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Show attempt interface error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async saveAnswer(attemptId, itemId, value) {
            const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
            const body = { operationId, itemId, value };
            
            try {
              await this.api('POST', `/api/attempts/${attemptId}/save-answer`, 
body);
              this.currentAttempt.answers = this.currentAttempt.answers || {};
              this.currentAttempt.answers[itemId] = value;
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Save answer error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async submitAttempt(attemptId) {
            if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to submit your attempt?')) 
return;
            
            const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
            const body = { operationId };
            
            try {
              const { data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/submit`, body);
              this.showStatus('Attempt submitted successfully', 'success');
              this.closeModal('attemptModal');
              await this.loadAttempts();
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Submit attempt error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async showAttemptSummary(attemptId, attempt) {
            let html = '<div>';
            
            html += `<p><strong>Status:</strong> ${attempt.status}</p>`;
            html += `<p><strong>Started:</strong> ${new 
Date(attempt.startedAt).toLocaleString()}</p>`;
            
            if (attempt.submittedAt) {
              html += `<p><strong>Submitted:</strong> ${new 
Date(attempt.submittedAt).toLocaleString()}</p>`;
            }
            
            if (attempt.feedbackStatus === 'released') {
              html += `<p><strong>Score:</strong> ${(attempt.objectiveScore || 
0) + (attempt.rubricScore || 0)}</p>`;
              if (attempt.grades) {
                html += '<div style="margin-top: 1rem;"><strong>Rubric 
Grades:</strong>';
                for (const grade of attempt.grades) {
                  html += `<div style="padding: 0.5rem; background: 
var(--bg-light); border-radius: 0.25rem; margin-top: 0.5rem;">`;
                  html += `<div><strong>${grade.criterion_id}</strong>: 
${grade.score} points</div>`;
                  html += `<div style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 
0.875rem;">${grade.feedback}</div>`;
                  html += `</div>`;
                }
                html += '</div>';
              }
            }
            
            html += '</div>';
            
            document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
attempt.assessmentTitle;
            document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;
            this.openModal('attemptModal');
          },
          
          async loadGradebook() {
            const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect3').value;
            if (!courseId) return;
            
            this.currentCourse = courseId;
            
            if (this.user.role === 'student') {
              document.getElementById('gradebookTabs').style.display = 'none';
              // Show student's own outcomes
              try {
                const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcomes`);
                // Students only see their own row
                // This is handled by the server
              } catch (error) {
                console.error('Load gradebook error:', error);
              }
            } else {
              document.getElementById('gradebookTabs').style.display = 'block';
              this.switchGradebookTab('outcomes');
            }
          },
          
          switchGradebookTab(tab) {
            document.getElementById('outcomesTab').style.display = tab === 
'outcomes' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('worksheetTab').style.display = tab === 
'worksheet' ? 'block' : 'none';
            document.getElementById('releaseTab').style.display = tab === 
'release' ? 'block' : 'none';
            
            if (tab === 'outcomes') {
              this.loadOutcomesLedger();
            } else if (tab === 'worksheet') {
              this.loadGradingWorksheet();
            } else if (tab === 'release') {
              this.loadBatchRelease();
            }
          },
          
          async loadOutcomesLedger() {
            const courseId = this.currentCourse;
            if (!courseId) return;
            
            try {
              const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/courses/${courseId}/outcomes`);
              
              if (!data || !data.outcomes) {
                document.getElementById('outcomesTab').innerHTML = '<p>No 
data</p>';
                return;
              }
              
              let html = '<table><thead><tr><th>Student</th>';
              for (const weight of data.weights) {
                html += `<th>${weight.assessment_id} (${(weight.weight * 
100).toFixed(0)}%)</th>`;
              }
              html += '<th>Grade</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
              
              for (const student of data.outcomes) {
                html += `<tr><td>${student.name}</td>`;
                
                for (const weight of data.weights) {
                  const assess = student.assessments[weight.assessment_id];
                  let cellContent = '—';
                  
                  if (assess.excused) {
                    cellContent = 'Excused';
                  } else if (assess.status === 'missing') {
                    cellContent = 'Missing';
                  } else if (assess.feedbackStatus !== 'released') {
                    cellContent = 'Pending';
                  } else if (assess.objectiveScore !== null) {
                    const max = 10; // TODO: calculate from items
                    cellContent = `${assess.objectiveScore + (assess.rubricScore
|| 0)}/${max}`;
                  }
                  
                  html += `<td>${cellContent}</td>`;
                }
                
                html += '<td>—</td>';
                html += '</tr>';
              }
              
              html += '</tbody></table>';
              document.getElementById('outcomesTab').innerHTML = html;
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Load outcomes error:', error);
            }
          },
          
          async loadGradingWorksheet() {
            const courseId = this.currentCourse;
            if (!courseId) return;
            
            try {
              const { data: assessments } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}`);
              
              let html = '<div>';
              
              for (const assessment of assessments) {
                if (assessment.status !== 'published') continue;

onclick="app.selectSubmissionsForGrading('${courseId}',

            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Load worksheet error:', error);
            }
          },

          async loadAudit() {
            const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect4').value;
            if (!courseId) return;
            
            try {
              const { data } = await this.api('GET', `/api/audit/${courseId}`);
              
              let html =

<tbody>';

          openModal(modalId) {
            document.getElementById(modalId).classList.add('open');
            document.getElementById(modalId).focus();
          },
          
          closeModal(modalId) {
            document.getElementById(modalId).classList.remove('open');
          },
          
          showStatus(message, type) {
            const status = document.getElementById('syncStatus');
            status.textContent = message;
            status.className = `sync-status show ${type}`;
            setTimeout(() => {
              status.classList.remove('show');
            }, 3000);
          }
        };
        
        // Initialize on load
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
          app.init();
        });
        
        // Handle modal close with Escape
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            document.querySelectorAll('.modal.open').forEach(m => {
              m.classList.remove('open');
            });
          }
        });
        
        // Handle reduced motion preference
        if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
          document.body.classList.add('prefers-reduced-motion');

+682|  <script>
+683|    // Global state
+684|    const app = {
+685|      token: localStorage.getItem('token'),
+686|      user: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}'),
+687|      currentCourse: null,
+688|      currentAssessment: null,
+689|      currentAttempt: null,
+690|      currentView: 'courses',
+691|      pendingOperations: new Map(),
+692|      courseRevisions: new Map(),
+693|      referenceMoment: new Date('2026-09-02T12:00:00Z'),
+694|      
+695|      async init() {
+696|        if (this.token) {
+697|          this.showSignedInUI();
+698|          await this.loadCourses();
+699|        } else {
+700|          this.showSignInUI();
+701|        }
+702|      },
+703|      
+704|      showSignInUI() {
+705|        document.getElementById('signInView').classList.add('active');
+706|        document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(nav => 
nav.style.display = 'none');
+707|        document.querySelector('header').style.display = 'none';
+708|        document.querySelectorAll('nav a').forEach(a => a.style.display = 
'none');
+709|      },
+710|      
+711|      showSignedInUI() {
+712|        document.getElementById('signInView').classList.remove('active');
+713|        document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(nav => 
nav.style.display = 'inline-block');
+714|        document.querySelector('header').style.display = 'flex';
+715|        document.getElementById('userName').textContent = this.user.name ||
'';
+716|        document.getElementById('userEmail').textContent = this.user.email 
|| '';
+717|      },
+718|      
+719|      async signIn(event) {
+720|        event.preventDefault();
+721|        const email = document.getElementById('emailInput').value;
+722|        const password = document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;
+723|        
+724|        try {
+725|          const response = await fetch('/api/auth/sign-in', {
+726|            method: 'POST',
+727|            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+728|            body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
+729|          });
+730|          
+731|          if (!response.ok) {
+732|            const data = await response.json();
+733|            document.getElementById('signInError').textContent = data.error
|| 'Sign in failed';
+734|            return;
+735|          }
+736|          
+737|          const data = await response.json();
+738|          this.token = data.token;
+739|          this.user = data.user;
+740|          localStorage.setItem('token', this.token);
+741|          localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(this.user));
+742|          
+743|          this.showSignedInUI();
+744|          await this.loadCourses();
+745|          this.switchView('courses');
+746|        } catch (error) {
+747|          document.getElementById('signInError').textContent = 'Network 
error: ' + error.message;
+748|        }
+749|      },
+750|      
+751|      async signOut() {
+752|        try {
+753|          await fetch('/api/auth/sign-out', {
+754|            method: 'POST',
+755|            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
+756|          });
+757|        } catch (error) {
+758|          console.error('Sign out error:', error);
+759|        }
+760|        
+761|        this.token = null;
+762|        this.user = {};
+763|        localStorage.removeItem('token');
+764|        localStorage.removeItem('user');
+765|        location.reload();
+766|      },
+767|      
+768|      async api(method, path, body = null) {
+769|        const opts = {
+770|          method,
+771|          headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${this.token}` }
+772|        };
+773|        
+774|        if (body) {
+775|          opts.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
+776|          opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
+777|        }
+778|        
+779|        const response = await fetch(path, opts);
+780|        if (response.status === 401) {
+781|          this.showSignInUI();
+782|          throw new Error('Not authenticated');
+783|        }
+784|        
+785|        const data = await response.json();
+786|        return { status: response.status, data };
+787|      },
+788|      
+789|      generateOperationId() {
+790|        return crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)).reduce((s, b) => 
s + b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'), '');
+791|      },
+792|      
+793|      async loadCourses() {
+794|        try {
+795|          const { data } = await this.api('GET', '/api/courses');
+796|          const html = data.length === 0 
+797|            ? '<div class="empty-state"><p>No courses enrolled</p></div>'
+798|            : data.map(c => `
+799|              <div class="card">
+800|                <h3 class="card-title">${c.title}</h3>
+801|                <p style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 0.875rem; margin: 
0.5rem 0 0 0;">${c.id}</p>
+802|              </div>
+803|            `).join('');
+804|          
+805|          document.getElementById('coursesList').innerHTML = html;
+806|          
+807|          // Populate course selects
+808|          const selects = ['courseSelect', 'courseSelect2', 
'courseSelect3', 'courseSelect4'];
+809|          for (const selectId of selects) {
+810|            const select = document.getElementById(selectId);
+811|            select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Choose a 
course...</option>';
+812|            for (const course of data) {
+813|              select.innerHTML += `<option 
value="${course.id}">${course.title}</option>`;
+814|            }
+815|          }
+816|        } catch (error) {
+817|          console.error('Load courses error:', error);
+818|        }
+819|      },
+820|      
+821|      switchView(view) {
+822|        this.currentView = view;
+823|        document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v => 
v.classList.remove('active'));
+824|        document.getElementById(view + 'View').classList.add('active');
+825|        document.querySelectorAll('nav a').forEach(a => 
a.removeAttribute('aria-current'));
+826|        document.querySelector(`nav 
a[data-view="${view}"]`).setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
+827|      },

+829|      async loadAssessments() {
+830|        const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect').value;
+831|        if (!courseId) return;
+832|        
+833|        this.currentCourse = courseId;
+834|        
+835|        try {
+836|          const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}`);
+837|          
+838|          let html = '';
+839|          if (data.length === 0) {
+840|            html = '<div class="empty-state"><p>No assessments</p></div>';
+841|          } else {
+842|            html = data.map(a => `
+843|              <div class="card">
+844|                <div class="card-header">
+845|                  <h3 class="card-title">${a.title}</h3>
+846|                  <span class="status-badge 
status-${a.status}">${a.status}</span>
+847|                </div>
+848|                <p><strong>Opens:</strong> ${new 
Date(a.opens_at).toLocaleString()}</p>
+849|                <p><strong>Due:</strong> ${new 
Date(a.due_at).toLocaleString()}</p>
+850|                <p><strong>Duration:</strong> ${a.duration_minutes} minutes
| <strong>Max Attempts:</strong> ${a.max_attempts}</p>
+851|                <div class="action-group">
+852|                  <button onclick="app.viewAssessment('${courseId}', 
'${a.id}')">View Details</button>
+853|                  ${this.user.role === 'instructor' && a.status === 'draft'
+854|                    ? `<button 
onclick="app.showEditDraftModal('${courseId}', '${a.id}')">Edit</button>` 
+855|                    : ''}
+856|                  ${this.user.role === 'student' && a.status === 
'published'
+857|                    ? `<button onclick="app.startAttempt('${courseId}', 
'${a.id}')">Start Attempt</button>`
+858|                    : ''}
+859|                </div>
+860|              </div>
+861|            `).join('');
+862|          }
+863|          
+864|          document.getElementById('assessmentsList').innerHTML = html;
+865|          document.getElementById('instructorActions').style.display = 
this.user.role === 'instructor' ? 'block' : 'none';
+866|        } catch (error) {
+867|          console.error('Load assessments error:', error);
+868|        }
+869|      },
+870|      
+871|      async viewAssessment(courseId, assessmentId) {
+872|        try {
+873|          const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}`);
+874|          
+875|          let itemsHtml = '';
+876|          for (const item of data.items) {
+877|            itemsHtml += `
+878|              <div class="question-card">
+879|                <div class="question-prompt">${item.prompt}</div>
+880|                ${item.kind === 'multiple_choice' 
+881|                  ? `<div style="font-size: 0.875rem; color: 
var(--gray);">Multiple Choice (${item.points} points)</div>` +
+882|                    item.options.map(o => `<div 
class="question-answer">${o}</div>`).join('')
+883|                  : `<div style="font-size: 0.875rem; color: 
var(--gray);">Written Response (${item.points} points)</div>`}
+884|                ${this.user.role !== 'student' && item.kind === 
'multiple_choice'
+885|                  ? `<div style="background: #fee2e2; padding: 0.5rem; 
border-radius: 0.25rem; margin-top: 0.5rem; font-size: 0.875rem;"><strong>Answer
Key:</strong> ${item.answer}</div>`
+886|                  : ''}
+887|              </div>
+888|            `;
+889|          }
+890|          
+891|          document.getElementById('assessmentDetailTitle').textContent = 
data.title;
+892|          document.getElementById('assessmentDetailContent').innerHTML = 
itemsHtml;
+893|          this.openModal('assessmentDetailModal');
+894|        } catch (error) {
+895|          console.error('View assessment error:', error);
+896|        }
+897|      },

+909|      async createDraft(event) {
+910|        event.preventDefault();
+911|        const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect').value;
+912|        if (!courseId) {
+913|          alert('Select a course first');
+914|          return;
+915|        }
+916|        
+917|        const title = document.getElementById('draftTitle').value;
+918|        const opens_at = new 
Date(document.getElementById('draftOpens').value).toISOString();
+919|        const due_at = new 
Date(document.getElementById('draftDue').value).toISOString();
+920|        const duration_minutes = 
parseInt(document.getElementById('draftDuration').value);
+921|        const max_attempts = 
parseInt(document.getElementById('draftAttempts').value);
+922|        
+923|        const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
+924|        const body = { operationId, title, opens_at, due_at, 
duration_minutes, max_attempts };
+925|        
+926|        try {
+927|          const { status, data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/create-draft`, body);
+928|          
+929|          if (status === 201) {
+930|            this.closeModal('createDraftModal');
+931|            this.showStatus('Draft created successfully', 'success');
+932|            await this.loadAssessments();
+933|          } else {
+934|            const errors = data.errors || [data.error];
+935|            document.getElementById('draftErrors').innerHTML = errors.map(e
=> `<div class="error-message">${e}</div>`).join('');
+936|          }
+937|        } catch (error) {
+938|          console.error('Create draft error:', error);
+939|        }
+940|      },
+941|      
+942|      async loadAttempts() {
+943|        const courseId = document.getElementById('courseSelect2').value;
+944|        if (!courseId) return;
+945|        
+946|        try {
+947|          const { data: assessments } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}`);
+948|          
+949|          let html = '';
+950|          for (const assessment of assessments) {
+951|            const { data: details } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessment.id}`);
+952|            if (details.items) {
+953|              html += `
+954|                <div class="card">
+955|                  <h3 class="card-title">${assessment.title}</h3>
+956|                  <div class="action-group">
+957|                    <button 
onclick="app.viewAttemptForAssessment('${courseId}', '${assessment.id}')">View 
Attempts</button>
+958|                  </div>
+959|                </div>
+960|              `;
+961|            }
+962|          }
+963|          
+964|          if (!html) {
+965|            html = '<div class="empty-state"><p>No assessments 
available</p></div>';
+966|          }
+967|          
+968|          document.getElementById('attemptsList').innerHTML = html;
+969|        } catch (error) {
+970|          console.error('Load attempts error:', error);
+971|        }
+972|      },
+973|      
+974|      async viewAttemptForAssessment(courseId, assessmentId) {
+975|        try {
+976|          const { data: assessment } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${courseId}/${assessmentId}`);
+977|          
+978|          let html = '<div>';
+979|          
+980|          if (this.user.role === 'student') {
+981|            const allAttempts = [];
+982|            let page = 0;
+983|            
+984|            const attemptList = 
localStorage.getItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`) || '[]';
+985|            const stored = JSON.parse(attemptList);
+986|            
+987|            for (const attempt of stored) {
+988|              try {
+989|                const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${attempt.id}`);
+990|                allAttempts.push(data);
+991|              } catch (error) {
+992|                // Skip this attempt
+993|              }
+994|            }
+995|            
+996|            if (allAttempts.length === 0) {
+997|              html += '<p>No attempts yet</p>';
+998|            } else {
+999|              html += 
'<table><thead><tr><th>Started</th><th>Status</th><th>Score</th><th>Action</th><
/tr></thead><tbody>';
+1000|              for (const attempt of allAttempts) {
+1001|                const score = attempt.status === 'in_progress' ? 'In 
progress' : 
+1002|                  attempt.feedbackStatus === 'released' ? 
`${(attempt.objectiveScore || 0) + (attempt.rubricScore || 0)}` : 'Not 
released';
+1003|                html += `<tr><td>${new 
Date(attempt.startedAt).toLocaleString()}</td><td><span class="status-badge 
status-${attempt.status}">${attempt.status}</span></td><td>${score}</td><td><but
ton onclick="app.resumeAttempt('${attempt.id}')">View</button></td></tr>`;
+1004|              }
+1005|              html += '</tbody></table>';
+1006|            }
+1007|          }
+1008|          
+1009|          html += '</div>';
+1010|          
+1011|          document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
`${assessment.title} - Attempts`;
+1012|          document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;
+1013|          this.openModal('attemptModal');
+1014|        } catch (error) {
+1015|          console.error('View attempt error:', error);
+1016|        }
+1017|      },
+1018|      
+1019|      async startAttempt(courseId, assessmentId) {
+1020|        const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
+1021|        const body = { operationId, assessmentId, expectedRevision: 
this.courseRevisions.get(courseId) || 0 };
+1022|        
+1023|        try {
+1024|          const { status, data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${courseId}/start`, body);
+1025|          
+1026|          if (status === 201) {
+1027|            const attempts = 
JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`) || '[]');
+1028|            attempts.push({ id: data.id });
+1029|            localStorage.setItem(`attempts_${assessmentId}`, 
JSON.stringify(attempts));
+1030|            
+1031|            await this.resumeAttempt(data.id);
+1032|          } else {
+1033|            alert(data.error || 'Failed to start attempt');
+1034|          }
+1035|        } catch (error) {
+1036|          console.error('Start attempt error:', error);
+1037|        }
+1038|      },
+1039|      
+1040|      async resumeAttempt(attemptId) {
+1041|        try {
+1042|          const { data } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}`);
+1043|          this.currentAttempt = data;
+1044|          
+1045|          if (this.user.role === 'student' && data.status === 
'in_progress') {
+1046|            await this.showAttemptInterface(attemptId, data);
+1047|          } else {
+1048|            await this.showAttemptSummary(attemptId, data);
+1049|          }
+1050|        } catch (error) {
+1051|          console.error('Resume attempt error:', error);
+1052|        }
+1053|      },
+1054|      
+1055|      async showAttemptInterface(attemptId, attempt) {
+1056|        try {
+1057|          const { data: assessment } = await this.api('GET', 
`/api/assessments/${attempt.assessmentId}`);
+1058|          
+1059|          let html = '<div>';
+1060|          
+1061|          // Timer
+1062|          const startTime = new Date(attempt.startedAt);
+1063|          const now = this.referenceMoment;
+1064|          const elapsed = (now - startTime) / 1000 / 60;
+1065|          const remaining = assessment.duration_minutes - elapsed;
+1066|          
+1067|          html += `<div style="background: var(--bg-light); padding: 1rem;
border-radius: 0.375rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">`;
+1068|          html += `<p style="margin: 0; font-size: 0.875rem; color: 
var(--gray);">Time Remaining</p>`;
+1069|          html += `<p class="countdown" style="margin: 0;">${Math.max(0, 
Math.ceil(remaining))} minutes</p>`;
+1070|          html += `</div>`;
+1071|          
+1072|          // Questions
+1073|          for (const item of assessment.items) {
+1074|            const answer = attempt.answers ? attempt.answers[item.id] : 
null;
+1075|            
+1076|            html += `<div class="question-card">`;
+1077|            html += `<div class="question-prompt">${item.prompt}</div>`;
+1078|            
+1079|            if (item.kind === 'multiple_choice') {
+1080|              html += `<div>`;
+1081|              for (const option of item.options) {
+1082|                const checked = answer === option ? 'checked' : '';
+1083|                html += `<label style="display: block; margin-bottom: 
0.5rem;"><input type="radio" name="item_${item.id}" value="${option}" ${checked}
onchange="app.saveAnswer('${attemptId}', '${item.id}', this.value)"> 
${option}</label>`;
+1084|              }
+1085|              html += `</div>`;
+1086|            } else {
+1087|              html += `<textarea name="item_${item.id}" style="min-height:
100px;" onchange="app.saveAnswer('${attemptId}', '${item.id}', 
this.value)">${answer || ''}</textarea>`;
+1088|            }
+1089|            
+1090|            html += `</div>`;
+1091|          }
+1092|          
+1093|          html += `<div class="action-group">`;
+1094|          html += `<button onclick="app.submitAttempt('${attemptId}')" 
style="background: var(--success); color: white; border: none;">Submit 
Attempt</button>`;
+1095|          html += `</div>`;
+1096|          html += `</div>`;
+1097|          
+1098|          document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
attempt.assessmentTitle;
+1099|          document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;
+1100|          this.openModal('attemptModal');
+1101|        } catch (error) {
+1102|          console.error('Show attempt interface error:', error);
+1103|        }
+1104|      },
+1105|      
+1106|      async saveAnswer(attemptId, itemId, value) {
+1107|        const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
+1108|        const body = { operationId, itemId, value };
+1109|        
+1110|        try {
+1111|          await this.api('POST', `/api/attempts/${attemptId}/save-answer`,
body);
+1112|          this.currentAttempt.answers = this.currentAttempt.answers || {};
+1113|          this.currentAttempt.answers[itemId] = value;
+1114|        } catch (error) {
+1115|          console.error('Save answer error:', error);
+1116|        }
+1117|      },
+1118|      
+1119|      async submitAttempt(attemptId) {
+1120|        if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to submit your attempt?')) 
return;
+1121|        
+1122|        const operationId = this.generateOperationId();
+1123|        const body = { operationId };
+1124|        
+1125|        try {
+1126|          const { data } = await this.api('POST', 
`/api/attempts/${attemptId}/submit`, body);
+1127|          this.showStatus('Attempt submitted successfully', 'success');
+1128|          this.closeModal('attemptModal');
+1129|          await this.loadAttempts();
+1130|        } catch (error) {
+1131|          console.error('Submit attempt error:', error);
+1132|        }
+1133|      },

+1135|      async showAttemptSummary(attemptId, attempt) {
+1136|        let html = '<div>';
+1137|        
+1138|        html += `<p><strong>Status:</strong> ${attempt.status}</p>`;
+1139|        html += `<p><strong>Started:</strong> ${new

+1140|        
+1141|        if (attempt.submittedAt) {
+1142|          html += `<p><strong>Submitted:</strong> ${new

+1143|        }
+1144|        
+1145|        if (attempt.feedbackStatus === 'released') {
+1146|          html += `<p><strong>Score:</strong> ${(attempt.objectiveScore ||

+1147|          if (attempt.grades) {
+1148|            html += '<div style="margin-top: 1rem;"><strong>Rubric 
Grades:</strong>';
+1149|            for (const grade of attempt.grades) {
+1150|              html += `<div style="padding: 0.5rem; background: 
var(--bg-light); border-radius: 0.25rem; margin-top: 0.5rem;">`;
+1151|              html += `<div><strong>${grade.criterion_id}</strong>: 
${grade.score} points</div>`;
+1152|              html += `<div style="color: var(--gray); font-size: 
0.875rem;">${grade.feedback}</div>`;
+1153|              html += `</div>`;
+1154|            }
+1155|            html += '</div>';
+1156|          }
+1157|        }
+1158|        
+1159|        html += '</div>';
+1160|        
+1161|        document.getElementById('attemptModalTitle').textContent = 
attempt.assessmentTitle;
+1162|        document.getElementById('attemptModalContent').innerHTML = html;
+1163|        this.openModal('attemptModal');
+1164|      },
+1165|

+1202|      async loadOutcomesLedger() {

+1209|          if (!data || !data.outcomes) {
+1210|            document.getElementById('outcomesTab').innerHTML = '<p>No

/api/assessments/:courseId/:assessmentId/publish** - Publish assessment 
(terminal, requires questions).\n\n## Student Attempts\n\n- **POST

application.\n- **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/save-answer** - Save student

in-progress answers).\n\n## Grading & Release\n\n- **POST 
/api/attempts/:attemptId/grade** - Grade single rubric criterion 
(instructor/assigned TA).\n- **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/grade-worksheet** -
Grade multiple criteria atomically.\n- **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/release**
- Release grades to student (instructor only, idempotent).\n- **GET 
/api/courses/:courseId/outcomes** - Get outcome ledger with weighted 
calculations (instructor only).\n\n## Outcome Management\n\n- **POST 
/api/courses/:courseId/outcome-weights** - Set assessment weights (must total 
100%, published only).\n- **POST /api/courses/:courseId/outcome-exception** -

/api/courses/:courseId/release-preview** - Create preview handle for selected

filtered audit trail (role-specific visibility).\n\n## Health Check\n\n- **GET 
/api/health** - No authentication required. Returns JSON health object.\n\n## 
Concurrency & Idempotency\n\n- Each course has an integer `revision` tracking 
data writes.\n- Stale-write requests return 409 with current revision.\n- All 
write operations require `operationId` (unpredictable string).\n- Duplicate 
operations return cached result without side effects.\n- Operation receipts 
stored with user, method, path, input hash in SQLite.\n\n## Privacy

    # Coursemark Application Manifest
    
    ## Start Command
    
    ```bash
    node /app/server.js
    ```
    
    Server runs on `0.0.0.0:3000`
    
    ## Database
    
    SQLite path: /app/coursemark.db
    
    ## Authentication
    
    - **POST /api/auth/sign-in** - Sign in with email and password. Returns 
bearer token and user.
    - **POST /api/auth/sign-out** - Sign out and revoke all sessions for the 
account.
    - Bearer tokens stored in SQLite `sessions` table.
    - All protected endpoints require `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header.
    
    ## Course Access
    
    - **GET /api/courses** - List courses user is enrolled in or instructing.
    - **GET /api/courses/:courseId** - Get single course with current revision.
    
    ## Assessment Authoring (Instructor Only)
    
    - **GET /api/assessments/:courseId** - List course assessments (students see
published only).
    - **GET /api/assessments/:courseId/:assessmentId** - Get assessment details 
with items.
    - **POST /api/assessments/:courseId/create-draft** - Create draft assessment
with title, opens_at, due_at, duration_minutes, max_attempts.
    - **POST /api/assessments/:courseId/:assessmentId/add-item** - Add question 
(multiple_choice or written) with validation.
    - **POST /api/assessments/:courseId/:assessmentId/publish** - Publish 
assessment (terminal, requires questions).
    
    ## Student Attempts
    
    - **POST /api/attempts/:courseId/start** - Start timed attempt with 
accommodation application.
    - **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/save-answer** - Save student answer 
(idempotent by operation ID).
    - **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/submit** - Submit attempt, calculate 
objective score, record audit event.
    - **GET /api/attempts/:attemptId** - Get attempt details (students see only 
their own in-progress answers).
    
    ## Grading & Release
    
    - **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/grade** - Grade single rubric criterion 
(instructor/assigned TA).
    - **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/grade-worksheet** - Grade multiple 
criteria atomically.
    - **POST /api/attempts/:attemptId/release** - Release grades to student 
(instructor only, idempotent).
    - **GET /api/courses/:courseId/outcomes** - Get outcome ledger with weighted
calculations (instructor only).
    
    ## Outcome Management
    
    - **POST /api/courses/:courseId/outcome-weights** - Set assessment weights 
(must total 100%, published only).
    - **POST /api/courses/:courseId/outcome-exception** - Excuse or restore 
student work.
    
    ## Batch Release
    
    - **POST /api/courses/:courseId/release-preview** - Create preview handle 
for selected attempts.
    - **POST /api/courses/:courseId/release-commit** - Atomically release all 
selected attempts.
    
    ## Audit
    
    - **GET /api/audit/:courseId** - Get filtered audit trail (role-specific 
visibility).
    
    ## Health Check
    
    - **GET /api/health** - No authentication required. Returns JSON health 
object.
    
    ## Concurrency & Idempotency
    
    - Each course has an integer `revision` tracking data writes.
    - Stale-write requests return 409 with current revision.
    - All write operations require `operationId` (unpredictable string).
    - Duplicate operations return cached result without side effects.
    - Operation receipts stored with user, method, path, input hash in SQLite.
    
    ## Privacy Enforcement
    
    - Unreleased rubric scores and feedback never appear in responses.
    - Students see only their own attempts and released data.
    - Teaching assistants see only assigned submissions.
    - Answer keys hidden from students.
    
    ## Data Persistence
    
    - SQLite with WAL mode for concurrent access.
    - Foreign key constraints enforced.
    - Seed data imported on first database creation.
    - All sessions, audit, and operation receipts survive restart.
